# Engine feature backlog

The detail behind the engine items in `BACKLOG.md` (the repo-root list of
everything still to do). This file ranks engine features by how many real
cards each would unblock, from two measured populations:

1. **The commander gap** (current priority): `top-commanders.txt` and the
   per-commander triage `top-commanders-gaps.json`, ranked by
   `npm run cmdrs:gaps -w engine`.
2. **The card backlog**: `top-commander-cards.txt` (the top 5000 Commander
   cards by EDHREC rank, refetched 2026-09-26, cross-referenced against `cards/pool/` — see
   `engine/scripts/top-commander-cards.mjs`).
   **Refresh the `[x]` marks with `npm run cards:mark -w engine`** after
   authoring; that re-marks in place and leaves the ranking snapshot alone.

Finished passes are indexed at the end, compactly, because source comments
cite them ("needed-cards P16", "Tier-1 feature"). Their narrative is in
`git log -- engine/src/cards/neededCards-features.md`. Read `cards/AUTHORING.md`
before authoring any card; this file is a priority list, not a how-to.

---

## The commander gap

(Card authoring's priority is now the Tarkir: Dragonstorm precons' missing cards, then the
top-5000 list — `BACKLOG.md`, "Card backlog". The commander numbers below date from 2026-09-25.)

**256 of the 500 most-played commanders are implemented** (2026-09-25).
Almost any real decklist someone imports still has its commander substituted,
which replaces the one card the deck is built around. `BACKLOG.md` carries
the current top of the build order; `cmdrs:gaps` has the live numbers.


### Why the pool missed them

Both existing backlogs rank by **how often a card is played across Commander
decks** — Scryfall's `order=edhrec`. That is the wrong measure for a
commander, and not slightly: a commander is a singleton in its own deck and
is played almost nowhere else, so its *card* rank stays low however popular
the deck is. Measured rather than assumed:

> Of EDHREC's 100 most-played commanders, **only 47 appear anywhere in the
> top 500 commander-legal cards by card rank.** Edgar Markov, The Ur-Dragon,
> Krenko, Kaalia, Yuriko and ~50 others are absent outright.

So no amount of working down `top-commander-cards.txt` would have fixed this.
It is a different population, and it needs its own list.

### The list

**`top-commanders.txt`** — the top 500 commanders by EDHREC `num_decks`: how
many decks run that card **as their commander**. Generated by
`npm run cmdrs:top -w engine`; re-mark after authoring with
`npm run cmdrs:mark -w engine` (no network, leaves the ranking snapshot put,
exactly like `cards:mark`).

Source caveat, recorded because it was a real decision: the ranking comes
from `json.edhrec.com`, which is **not a documented API** — the same
objection that kept the card replacer off Scryfall Tagger's GraphQL. It is
used here because nothing else publishes commander-specific deck counts, and
because this is a *build-time* dependency producing a checked-in file: if the
endpoint changes, the list still works and only a re-fetch breaks. Names,
costs and type lines still come from Scryfall. Partner pairs, which EDHREC
files under a joined "A // B" name that looks exactly like a double-faced
card, are told apart by asking Scryfall (a DFC resolves, a pair doesn't) and
credited to each half.

### What it takes — measured, not screened

The first version of this section read a keyword screen as "authoring, not
engine work". **That was wrong.** On 2026-09-22 every unimplemented entry was
triaged individually. One agent per eight commanders read each card's Oracle
text and rulings against the engine, and named clause by clause what it
would need. The ad-hoc gap names were then normalized onto one vocabulary of
245 engine features. The result is checked in as
**`top-commanders-gaps.json`** (commander → features, feature → size / needs
client UI / what to build). **`npm run cmdrs:gaps -w engine`** re-ranks it,
leaving out commanders already marked implemented and features listed in its
`built` array. So record a feature there when it lands, and the numbers stay
current.

Of the 469 still missing when the snapshot was taken:

| features still needed | 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5+ |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| commanders | 29 | 85 | 124 | 112 | 71 | 48 |

- **~94% need engine work, but it's a long tail of small primitives, not a
  few big mechanics.** No feature blocks more than 22 of them, and even the
  most common rarely block one alone. The blockers are unglamorous: a static
  scope that takes a filter, last-known information, "another target",
  dynamic comparisons in filters, per-turn counters. This is the Tier 1b
  lesson again, at the scale of the whole commander list.
- **Greedy engine-only order.** Repeatedly build the feature that fully
  unblocks the most commanders per unit of effort (small 1, medium 3,
  large 10), leaving out anything that needs client UI. That unblocks about
  **75 commanders with 40 features**, most of them small. `cmdrs:gaps` prints
  the current order.

The live per-feature ranking (needed by, sole blocker, size, needs client
UI) and the greedy order are `cmdrs:gaps`' output; each feature's "what to
build" — including what is already built of a partly shipped one — is its
`description` in the JSON. The commanders authored and then dropped by their
reviews carry a `note` there, and are listed in `BACKLOG.md`.

### Suggested order

1. **Build down `cmdrs:gaps`' engine-only order**, authoring each
   commander a feature unblocks in the same commit, each with an
   adversarial rules review.
2. **Then the UI-bound features.** Ward payment (choosing new targets for a
   copy landed 2026-09-30), "may sacrifice — when you do", choosing a permanent, and entering
   attacking with a chosen defender each need a new decision the client
   renders. See also `docs/plans/token-stack-choices.md`.

## Open: the card backlog

Ranked from a keyword screen of the top 2000 taken on 2026-09-12. The screen
itself is retired: the card sweeps' per-card triage replaced it ("Card sweep
1" below, and `engine/data/sweep-2/`). Tier 1 and the Tier 1b primitives are
done (see the index at the end). Measure before picking the next one: `node
scripts/top-commander-cards.mjs --count 2000 --out <throwaway> --cache-json
<path>` gives current Oracle text for the whole list without touching the
checked-in snapshot.

### Tier 2 — solid value, smaller or more speculative

- ~~**Spree**~~ *(built — five Spree cards in the pool; Return the Favor and Great Train Heist wait on other features, BACKLOG's engine rules gaps)*.
- **Class enchantments** — **built 2026-10-10** (rule 716: `classLevel(n, cost)`, `atLevel(n, ability)`, the `class-level-gained` trigger; batch 45, 13 cards — 22 were waiting). The 9 left each need a clause of their own (`B45.json`).

### Tier 3 — real but niche, or a big lift for a small current payoff

- **Station** (7 cards, mostly newest-set/low-rank: Exploration Broodship, Evendo, Uthros) — a whole new subsystem (a permanent sub-type + counter-threshold-gated text tiers, keyed off what's tapped to fund it). Large build, currently low return — revisit once more Station cards enter the format.
- **Discover** (4), **Reconfigure** (3) — each a distinct, self-contained alt-cast/alt-ability shape; neither shares much with the other or with Tier 1/2. Cherry-pick opportunistically. (**Evoke** is built for mana costs, 2026-09-29; the Incarnations' exile-a-card evoke cost is BACKLOG's.)
- **Phasing** (8 cards, headlined by **Teferi's Protection** at #109) — explicitly out of scope per this file's parent (CLAUDE.md's "Not modeled" list). High-profile but a genuinely large state-machine addition (a whole not-really-a-zone permanent status); revisit only as a deliberate scope change, not opportunistically.
- **Vehicles/crew** — **built 2026-10-09** (`crew(n)`, rule 702.122; batch 38, 24 cards — it led the triage records with 38 cards waiting). Left: "becomes crewed" triggers, "crews as though its power were greater" and "crewed it this turn".
- **Casting during resolution** — **mostly built 2026-10-09** (`cast-now`'s `copies`, rule 707.12/704.5e; any number from among exiled cards; a top X amount; batch 41, 14 cards). Left, each its own key (`B41.json`): search to exile then cast (4 cards), an alternative cost, another player casting, the command zone, total mana value, any mana type, the discarded card.
- **Ninjutsu** — **built 2026-10-09** (`ninjutsu(cost)`, `commanderNinjutsu(cost)`, rule 702.49; batch 43, 15 cards — 23 were waiting). Left: a static granting ninjutsu to cards in a hand and "whenever you activate a ninjutsu ability" (Satoru Umezawa), "enters from your hand" (Thousand-Faced Shadow).
- **Rooms** — **built 2026-10-09** (rule 709.5: `roomCard`, `unlockThisDoor`, the `unlock-door` special action and effect; batch 44, 13 cards — 20 were waiting). The 7 left each need a clause of their own (`B44.json`).
- **Dungeons/Initiative** (3), **split/aftermath layout** (4), **Battle cards** (2) — each explicitly out of scope already; low card counts in the top 2000 confirm they're not worth a scope change yet.
- **Backgrounds** (3) are no longer blocked as a mechanic: the pairing is built (`pairing: { kind: "choose-a-background" }`) and one Background, Raised by Giants, is authored. What's left of the three comes down to each card's own text.

### Still open inside finished features

- **Overload**: Mizzix's Mastery, Eldritch Immunity, Winds of Abandon (a
  different effect shape each).
- **Conditional free-cast**: Deflecting Swat (targeting a stack *ability* and
  a change-the-target effect); Etali, Primal Storm (multi-card impulse +
  cast-any-number).
- **Damage doubling** is built (`would-deal-damage`): Twinflame Tyrant, City
  on Fire and Fiery Emancipation were authored 2026-09-29. Solphim still
  waits on "Discard two cards" as a cost.
- **Convoke**: Clever Concealment (phasing).
- **Additional costs**: an optional, repeatable cost — Plumb the Forbidden,
  Dargo ("sacrifice one or more creatures"). Redirect Lightning needs a
  change-the-target effect.
- **Global land-type statics**: Urborg, Tomb of Yawgmoth and Yavimaya, Cradle
  of Growth — `effectiveSubtypes` is deliberately given no `GameState`, so a
  board-dependent land subtype touches check lands and every `subtype`
  clause.
- **Remaining `StaticCondition`s**: Coven and Raid (no card whose other text
  is expressible yet).
- **Protection from a filter** (19 cards — Mother of Runes, Giver of Runes,
  the Sword cycle): `protection: {colors, types}` widening to a `CardFilter`.
  The One Ring and Teferi's Protection give protection to a *player* instead.
- **Cards E3 skipped** for want of a primitive and still unauthored:
  Ponder (put cards back in any order), The One Ring (protection for a
  player), Urza's Saga. (Chaos Warp's `shuffle-into-library` was built
  2026-10-02.)

### Modal triggers with targeted modes — built 2026-09-26

**Built** as `ModeOption.targets` on an `announced` modal effect: the chosen
modes bring their targets as the ability goes on the stack, a mode needing a
target it can't have isn't offered (rule 603.3c), and each mode reads its own
slice of the targets as it resolves (`withTargetSlice`), so a mode whose
target has gone still does the rest while an all-illegal ability does nothing
(608.2b). "Choose one that hasn't been chosen this turn" now works announced
too, recorded as each copy goes on the stack. Measured before: 32 missing
top-5000 cards. Shipped: Hullbreaker Horror, Junji, Aether Channeler, Retreat
to Coralhelm, Charming Prince, Silverback Elder, Retreat to Kazandu, Dread
Presence, Pip-Boy 3000, Voracious Hydra (`double-counters` gained a
`counter` kind), Retreat to Hagra, Shambling Ghast, Tiller Engine, Oltec
Matterweaver, Kykar, Glorious Sunrise, and the commanders Ayula, Queen Among
Bears, Jin Sakai, Ghost of Tsushima and Galadriel, Light of Valinor, with
Breeches and Parapet Thrasher. Still blocked among the 32: shield counters
(Titan of Industry), countering an ability (Ertai Resurrected), "remove up to
three counters" of the player's choice (Glissa Sunslayer), a mode never chosen
before, not just this turn (Silent Hallcreeper), mana spent as though any
colour (Grenzo), a "life they lost this turn" read for a *target* player
(Astarion — `turnStat` has no target scope), modes that must target
different players (Shadrix), Role tokens (Charming Scoundrel), a
nonartifact-spell mana restriction (The Mightstone and Weakstone), a card
shuffled in from the graveyard (Kogla and Yidaro), and Caesar's attacking
tokens. Modal *activated* abilities (Breya, Koma, Umezawa's Jitte) are
`effect:modal-activated-targeted-modes` in the gaps file.

### Host triggers — built 2026-09-26

**Built** as the `"attached"` `TriggerWho`: "whenever **equipped** creature
…", "whenever **enchanted** creature / land …" — the Equipment's or Aura's
host as the event happened, whoever controls it, with the trigger still the
attachment's and its controller's. A host that died is matched by the link
the attachment still has until the next state-based check (704.5n); an
attachment that left alongside it remembers its host
(`LastKnownInfo.attachedTo`, rule 603.10a). Measured before it was built: 75
missing top-5000 cards. It shipped with Skullclamp, Wild Growth (a triggered
mana ability, so the auto-payer counts it), Sword of the Animist, Sword of
Feast and Famine, Sword of Fire and Ice, Sword of Light and Shadow,
Curiosity, Ophidian Eye, Aqueous Form, Mask of Memory, Goldvein Pick,
Beamtown Beatstick, Argentum Armor, Explorer's Scope and Sticky Fingers.

A second batch followed the same day: Winged Boots, Bloodforged Battle-Axe,
Super State, Sword of Truth and Justice (its "a creature you control" is a
resolution-time `choose-permanents`), Kaya's Ghostform, Keen Sense,
Overgrowth, Wolfwillow Haven, Rogue's Gloves, Zephyr Boots, Sword of Body and
Mind, Sword of Sinew and Steel, Sword of War and Peace, Quietus Spike, Ultima
Weapon, Staff of Titania, Wand of Orcus, Adaptive Omnitool, Bilbo's Ring, The
Spear of Leonidas, Fiendlash, Sigil of Sleep, Resurrection Orb and Mage Slayer
(an attack trigger's `toTriggerRecipient` is now the player or planeswalker
attacked). 28 of the 75 are still blocked (34 then; Pip-Boy 3000, Shiny Impetus, Sword of Forge and
Frontier, Sword of Wealth and Power, Fertile Ground and Vorpal Sword have since
been authored), each by something outside host triggers, most of it shared
with other cards:

- **A static "is goaded"** (the Impetus Auras: Parasitic, Martial,
  Ghoulish): goad as a continuous effect of an attachment, not a one-shot.
- **"Return this card" after its host died** (Angelic Destiny, Endless Evil,
  Gift of Immortality, Ghoulish Impetus): the Aura reaches the graveyard by the
  state-based check after its trigger, so the ability has to follow that one
  move, the way a leaves-the-battlefield trigger follows its own object.
- **"Deals damage" once per event** (Spirit Link, Umezawa's Jitte, Lost
  Jitte): with no recipient named, damage to several things at once is one
  trigger (rule 603.2c), where `deals-damage` fires once per recipient.
- **Free casts during resolution** (Buster Sword, Sword of Once and Future, The
  Key to the Vault), **tokens that enter tapped and attacking** (Andúril, Flame of
  the West), **For Mirrodin!** (Hexplate Wallbreaker; living weapon and
  "the first combat phase of the turn" are built — Genji Glove and
  Nettlecyst are authored), **ascend** (Andúril, Narsil Reforged), **stun
  counters** (Mjölnir), a **dynamic "pay X life"** (Mask of Griselbrand), an
  **attached host condition** (Combat Research's "as long as enchanted
  creature is legendary"), and one-offs (Sword of Hearth and Home, Songbirds'
  Blessing, Infiltration Lens, Thran Power Suit, Pain for All,
  Ordeal of Nylea, The Aetherspark).

Sword of Fire and Ice turned up a real bug: a combat damage trigger's first
target slot was auto-filled with the damaged player whenever it could hold
one, so Mindscour Dragon's "target player mills four" could only ever pick
the damaged opponent. "That player" is now the `"trigger-player"` scope
(Xyris, Captain N'ghathrod moved to it) and every slot is a real choice.

### Hexproof from abilities, and a "can't be the target" that isn't hexproof — built 2026-11-03

`keyword:hexproof-from-abilities`: Volatile Stormdrake's keyword is `hexproofFrom: { abilitiesOnly: true }` (lost with
hexproof, 702.11e); the printed "can't be the target of …" statics take `notHexproof` and keep through a loss of
hexproof (Shanna, Sisay's Legacy against Shadowspear — and Dense Foliage, Fiendslayer Paladin, Mercenary Informer,
Thrun, marked the same). Proving cards: Volatile Stormdrake, Shanna, Sisay's Legacy.

### A player becoming a target, an ability's targets, the first time by anyone — built 2026-11-03

`trigger:player-becomes-target`, `trigger:becomes-target-by-your-ability`, `trigger:becomes-target-first-time-by-anyone`
(family `trigger:becomes-target-fixes`): the `player-targeted` event (once per player a spell or ability targets),
`becomes-target`'s `player`, `playerOnly`, `abilityOnly` and `firstTimeEachTurnByAnyone`. Proving cards: Amulet of
Safekeeping, Unsettled Mariner, Dormant Gomazoa, Loki, God of Mischief, Angelic Cub. Still needing more: Leyline of
Combustion ("you and/or at least one permanent you control" — once per spell or ability), Rayne's "if enchanted",
Parnesse's copy, Skophos Maze-Warden's land named Labyrinth of Skophos, Kira's granted "counter that spell or
ability"; `trigger:becomes-target-spell-on-stack` (Surrak) and `-of-ability-targeting-only-it` (Agrus Kos) open.

### A land tapped for mana producing another type — built 2026-11-03

`replacement:tap-for-mana-produces-color-instead`: `StaticAbility.landManaInstead` (per land kind, whoever taps or
only you) and the `player-effect` field `landManaInstead` (until end of turn), applied where a land's {T} mana
ability makes mana, in the auto-payer's options and in the offered activations. Proving cards: Deep Water, Reality
Twist, Infernal Darkness. Harvest Mage's "one mana of a color of your choice instead of any other type and amount"
still needs the choice and the amount.

### Unspent mana as steps and phases end: another type, every player's kept, life lost — built 2026-11-03

`replacement:mana-pool-emptying` (family `replacement:mana-pool-emptying`): `unspentManaBecomes` (Kruphix's
replacement to a colour — Omnath, Locus of All, Ozai), `keepsUnspentManaEachPlayer` (Upwelling) and
`manaLossCausesLifeLoss` (Yurlok of Scorch Thrash), in the rule 500.4 pool emptying. Proving cards: Horizon Stone,
Upwelling, Yurlok of Scorch Thrash. Still open: `replacement:mana-persistence-color` (The Last Agni Kai's one-shot
"until end of turn, you don't lose unspent red mana" beside its excess-fight-damage mana).

### A draw replaced by an effect, and draw-N exemptions — built 2026-11-03

`replacement:draw-replacement-look-and-choose`, `replacement:draw-replacement-except-first`,
`replacement:draw-replacement-first-each-turn` (family `replacement:new-event-replacements`).
`would-draw`'s `instead: { effect }` (rule 614.11, 614.11a: each draw of several replaced and
answered before the next, applied on an empty library), and `exceptFirstInDrawStep` /
`firstEachTurn` on `draws` and `effect` replacements, the turn's draw events kept in
`PlayerState.wouldDrawThisTurn`. Proving cards: Underrealm Lich, Tomorrow, Azami's Familiar,
Sages of the Anima, Teferi's Ageless Insight, Reed Richards, Smartest Man, Bard, King of Dale.
Still open in the group: `replacement:draw-replacement-reveal` (Abundance's optional
land-or-nonland reveal, Breathstealer's Crypt's draw-and-reveal, the play-with-hand-revealed
pair). Scion of Halaster needs its replacement granted to commander creatures; Aladdin's Lamp a
one-shot "the next time you would draw" shield.

### An exile's end as a parameter — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `effect:exile-from-hand-until-source-leaves` +
`effect:exile-until-opponent-becomes-monarch` + `effect:exile-until-other-target-leaves`,
all rule 610.3: `discard-chosen`'s `untilSourceLeaves` (back to the hand it came from,
`exiledUntil.returnTo`), and `exile`'s `until`, `{ targetLeaves: N }` or
`"opponent-becomes-monarch"` (`GameObject.exiledUntilMonarch`, read in `Game.setMonarch`;
`GameState.monarchBecameAt` for 610.3b). Proving cards: Kitesail Freebooter, Brain Maggot,
Deep-Cavern Bat, Palace Jailer, Calix. Left over from the first key: Cloak and Dagger,
Entwined (one choice over a hand and a creature), Intellect Devourer (each opponent's own
choice, and playing them), Hypnox and Wormfang Behemoth (a whole hand exiled at once).
Mesmeric Fiend reads as Tidehollow Sculler's shape, no new code. The family's next unit is
the "until your next turn" durations.

### Held down through a chosen player's next N untap steps — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `effect:doesnt-untap-next-two-untap-steps` +
`effect:doesnt-untap-next-untap-step` + `effect:doesnt-untap-your-next-untap-step`:
`tap`'s `doesntUntapSteps` (and `tap-all`'s) and `untapStepOf: "you"`, on
`GameObject.skipsFurtherUntaps` and a `skipsUntapOf` that holds one `{ player, steps }`
a player. Overlapping holds keep the longer rather than add. Proving cards: Telekinesis,
Reveka, Samite Alchemist, Spectral Bears, Vorinclex, Dreamshackle Geist; the ten Tempest
lands read "your next untap step" exactly now. Left over: `Deep Spawn`'s and `Homarid
Warrior`'s effects are expressible, only the Homarid's "Tap it" wants the parser's help.

### Attacking and blocking alone, or not — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:can-only-attack-alone` (its group's four
keys, and `static:cant-attack-alone` riding along), features only, no new
decision kind. Five `CombatRestriction`s, so an Aura or a `restrict` carries them
as it does any other; `combat/company.ts` says what each asks of the others
declared. The offers leave out a creature with no group it could be in, and carry
`company` / `blockCompany`; `attackCompanyViolations` and
`blockCompanyViolations` go beside the validators' other set-level checks. With
company in play, attack requirements are counted as rule 508.1d says (the most
obeyed, not all), so "another must attack, so this can't" holds, and
`withinAttackCompany` / `withinBlockCompany` fix a bot's or the fuzzer's
declaration without a draw. 5 proving cards: Mogg Flunkies, Craven Hulk, Orcish
Conscripts, Errantry, Bonded Construct. Left open: Okk's and Scarred Puma's "a
creature with greater power / a black or green creature also attacks" (their own
keys), the "no more than N creatures can attack" side of
`static:attack-limit`. The client's Confirm isn't told: an illegal declaration is
refused with the engine's reason, as a toast.

### Limits on the untap step — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:untap-step-limit`, features only, no new
decision kind. **`untapLimit`** (a static: `who`, `count`, `filter?`) and
**`untapOnlyChosenType`** (`who`, `types`) decide which permanents untap in
rule 502.3's step, after "you may choose not to": `Game.continueUntapStep` asks
each Storage Matrix's question as a `choose-modes` (`awaiting.untapTypes`), then
each limit still broken as a `choose-permanents` of exactly `count`
(`awaiting.untapLimit`), and only then untaps. A limit is a maximum on what
would otherwise untap, so the player untaps all it allows (502.3: only an effect
keeps a permanent tapped). A partial stack's tokens split off. Reused the
decisions, so the client needs nothing. 6 proving cards: Winter Orb, Static Orb,
Winter Moon, Smoke, Mungha Wurm, Storage Matrix. An emblem's limit is reached too
(tested on a bare emblem, for Dovin Baan's).

### The owner, not the controller, of a target or trigger object — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 group of `effect:owner-of-target-player-ref` in the
family `effect:target-spec-additions`, features only. `{ ownerOfTarget }`
reaches `gain-life`, `lose-life`, `damage`'s `who`, `mill` and
`exile-graveyard`, beside `draw` and `discard`, which took it already. The new
`"trigger-owner"` scope is "that player" of "returned to a player's hand" and
"put into a player's graveyard" (rule 108.3: an owner never changes, and a
token's is read as it last existed). `damage`'s `who: "attached-owner"` with
`from: "attached"` is "enchanted creature deals 1 damage to its owner". The
scaffolder reads each line and both triggers. Keys:
effect:owner-of-target-player-ref, effect:exile-graveyard-of-target-card-owner,
effect:trigger-object-owner-player-ref. Proving cards: Path of Peace, Recoil,
Geth, Nurgle's Conscription, Enslave, Warped Devotion, Liability.

### A cost keyed to a spell's target: a mana tax, and a reduction on the target's controller — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:target-controller-graveyard-reduction`
with `cost:targeted-by-spells-mana-tax`, features only. Terror of the Peaks'
`targetedBySpellsCost` takes `payGeneric` beside `payLife` ("cost {2} more to
cast"), can be granted (`grantsStatic`: one instance per permanent named), and
with `targeting` reads the spell's targets against a filter ("a Merfolk you
control", "one or more commanders you control"), once per spell. Kopala's other
half is `abilityCostModification`'s `byOpponents` and `targeting`. The total
is fixed once the targets are (rules 601.2c, 601.2f): `Game.castingCostOf`
prices the chosen targets, an increase before any reduction. An offer prices
each target through a probe (`targetTaxOffer`) and leaves out what its caster
can't afford, says the rest in `LegalAction.targetTaxes` and
`xCost.targetTaxes`, and the drivers fit to them (`targetsWithinTaxes`,
`maxXForTargets`). The new filter clause `controllerGraveyardAtLeast` is
Lullmage's Domination's target; a `targetCostReduction` whose filter reads X
is now priced at each X's own targets. The scaffolder reads each line. Keys:
cost:target-controller-graveyard-reduction, cost:targeted-by-spells-mana-tax.
Proving cards: Boreal Elemental, Jubilant Skybonder, Kopala, Lullmage's
Domination.
### A P/T bonus by each affected creature's own count, and other amounts — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:pt-per-amount`, features only. On
`grantPtPerCount`: `relativeToAffected` asks `filter` of each affected permanent
(`attachedTo: { isSource: true }` is "attached to it", `excludeSelf` "other than
it"), `sharesCreatureTypeWithAffected` narrows the filter to its creature types,
and `ofAffected` is its mana value, colors or creature types. Then `countersAmong`,
`playerCountersOf: "opponents"`, `anyCountersOnSource`, `turnStat`, `basicLandTypes`
(Kavu Scout, Wayfaring Giant, Strength of Unity and Exotic Curse moved to it from
five statics each), `opponents`, `fromAffectedController`, `step` and `max`. The
greatest mana value among a set was already `aggregate`. The scaffolder reads the
lines. Keys: static:count-attached-to-affected,
static:pt-per-count-excluding-affected, static:shares-creature-type-count,
static:grant-pt-equal-attached-mana-value, static:grant-pt-per-own-colors,
static:pt-per-affected-creature-types, static:pt-per-counters-among-permanents,
static:pt-per-opponents-player-counters, static:pt-per-turn-stat,
static:grant-pt-per-any-counter-on-source, static:grant-pt-per-domain,
static:pt-bonus-greatest-mana-value,
static:pt-per-count-affected-controller-graveyard, static:pt-per-count-step,
static:pt-per-opponent. Proving cards: Thran Power Suit, Auramancer's Guise,
Champion of the Flame, Sliver Legion, Coat of Arms, Alpha Status, Hedron Matrix,
Civic Saber, Knight of New Alara, Mycosynth Fiend, Gleam of Authority, Knowledge Is
Power, Kyler, Manaforce Mace, Dark Matter Manipulator, Disturbing Conversion. Left:
mechanic:hone-counters, static:pt-per-turn-history (Kinbinding),
static:anthem-per-counter-on-source (Banner of Kinship's enters-with count),
static:per-keyword-pump, static:pt-per-attachments-on-source,
static:pt-per-defending-player-count.

### Protection from players, everything, spells, subtypes and exiled card types — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 group `keyword:protection-from-opponents` and its six
others, features only: protection (rule 702.16) whose quality is any source
quality, read by all four parts — target, enchant and equip, damage, block —
through `qualityMatches`. New: `fromPlayers` on a static, a player's protection
and `grant-protection` (702.16k: "opponents", fixed as a grant resolves, or
"chosen"), `CardFilter.isSpell` ("spells that are one or more colors", "instant
spells"), `exiledCardTypes`, a static's filter read with its own source (Riders
of Gavony's "of the chosen type"), `doesntRemoveSource` by card type (702.16n),
everything needing no object to ask about (702.16j), a source that left read as
it last existed for a filter, and `protectionLabel` in words instead of "a
quality" (`protection-qualities.test.ts`). Keys:
keyword:protection-from-opponents, keyword:protection-from-everything,
keyword:protection-from-colored-spells,
keyword:protection-from-spells-and-cast-permanents,
keyword:protection-from-subtype, static:protection-from-exiled-card-types,
static:protection-except-this-aura; Tier B static:player-protection-from-opponents,
filter:protection-from-spells-only. Proving cards: Cliffside Rescuer, Figure of
Fable, Absolute Virtue, True-Name Nemesis, Vexilus Praetor, Emrakul the Aeons Torn,
Emrakul the World Anew, Devoted Caretaker, Baneslayer Angel, Riders of Gavony,
Mirror Golem, Tattoo Ward. Left: protection from mana values and from a target
player (groups of their own), the bots' `combat-math` reading only colours and
types.

### A source of your choice, to anything, for the turn — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `replacement:prevent-damage-from-chosen-source`,
features only. Most of it was in `prevent-damage-from-chosen-source` already
(`to: "any"`, `amount: "all"`, a redirection, rule 609.7), with no card, test or
parser line reading it that way. New: `to: "your-side-creatures"` ("you and/or
creatures you control") and `gainLifeFrom`, the filter on the source a
`gainLifePrevented` reads as the damage would be dealt
(`chosen-source-recipients.test.ts`); the scaffolder reads each wording, and "you
gain life …" after one. Keys:
replacement:prevent-all-damage-from-chosen-source-this-turn,
replacement:prevent-next-damage-from-chosen-source-any-recipient,
replacement:redirect-chosen-source-any-recipient,
replacement:chosen-source-shield-you-and-your-creatures. Proving cards: Burrenton
Forge-Tender, Prahv, Pilgrim of Virtue, Opal-Eye, Samite Ministration, Shadowbane.
Left: Mourner's Shield needs "shares a color with the exiled card" as a filter.

### Keyword abilities and types for cards outside the battlefield — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:grant-to-cards-outside-battlefield`,
features only. `grantsToGraveyard.keywords` gives cards in your graveyard
unearth, encore, embalm, harmonize, retrace or sneak, each with its cost
(`GraveyardKeywordGrant`: fixed, `"mana-cost"`, or `"mana-value"` for "encore
{X}, where X is its mana value"), read by `Game.graveyardKeywordsOf`; an emblem
holds such a grant too (rule 113.6p). `grant-graveyard-keyword` is the one-shot,
until end of turn, for one target or each card matching a filter
(`GameObject.grantedGraveyardKeywords`). `castWithSneakFromGraveyard` is Ninja
Teen's "using their sneak abilities". `grantsCyclingInHand` (`search`,
`eachPlayer`) and `grantsForetellInHand` (`reducedBy`, the cost kept on the card
as `foretoldCost`) are the hand's, with the `foretells` trigger.
`typesOutsideBattlefield` (`ofType`, `zones`) is layer 4 off the battlefield,
read through `layerFour` by every filter, and `setsChosenCreatureType` is
"are the chosen type" with no "in addition". The scaffolder reads them all.
Keys: effect:grant-embalm, effect:grant-harmonize,
effect:grant-unearth-to-graveyard-until-eot, static:grant-encore-in-graveyard,
static:grant-sneak-to-graveyard, static:grant-unearth-to-graveyard,
static:graveyard-cards-gain-chosen-type, static:static-types-outside-battlefield,
static:grant-cycling-to-cards-in-hand, static:grant-foretell,
static:retrace-grant. Proving cards: Sedris, the Traitor King; Solemn Doomguide;
Wire Surgeons; Graywater's Fixer; Cursecloth Wrappings; Songcrafter Mage; Ninja
Teen; Deeproot Historian; Six; Wrenn and Six; Rhet-Tomb Mystic; Tectonic
Reformation; Homing Sliver; Dream Devourer; Ashes of the Fallen; Arcane
Adaptation; Conspiracy; Maskwood Nexus. Left: Ghost Ark's "becomes crewed"
trigger, Ethereal Valkyrie's "exile a card from your hand face down. It becomes
foretold", Araumi's cost, Arcade Gannon (static:graveyard-cast-filter-amount).

### Activations this turn: a limit of N, or of a count, and a condition on the count — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:ability-activated-count-this-turn`,
features only. An activated ability's `timesEachTurn` is "activate no more
than twice each turn" (a number) or "no more times each turn than the
number of snow Swamps you control" (a `CostReductionAmount` count, read as
it would be activated): rule 602.5b, kept with `oncePerTurn`'s record on
`GameObject.abilitiesUsedThisTurn`, one entry an activation, so it stays
with the object through a change of control. A mana ability with it is left
out of the auto-payer (activated by hand). The new `StaticCondition`
`activated-this-turn` (`atLeast`) is "if this ability has been activated
four or more times this turn", read inside the ability's own `conditional`
off `GameState.abilityActivationsThisTurn` (keyed like
`abilityResolutionsThisTurn`, counted as each activation is made, only for
an ability whose effect asks). The scaffolder reads both limit lines and the
condition's "if". Keys: condition:ability-activated-count-this-turn,
cost:activate-no-more-than-n-times-each-turn, cost:activation-limit-by-count.
Proving cards: Pit Imp, Sewer Rats, Withering Wisps, Dragon Whelp.

### Can't play lands, and casting barred by zone — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:cast-and-activate-restrictions`'s
first unit, features only. The `prohibit` effect takes `lands: true`
("target player can't play lands this turn" — rule 305.2, a land from any
zone; a `turnProhibitions` entry read by `whyLandPlayProhibited`). A static
`prohibits` takes `from` / `notFrom` (zone lists) binding its `spells` and
`lands` only to a card in, or outside, those zones as the cast or play
begins (rule 601.2a — `prohibitionReachesZone`): "your opponents can't cast
spells from anywhere other than their hands" (`notFrom: ["hand"]`), "you
can't play lands or cast spells from your hand" (`from: ["hand"]`). A
`player-effect`'s `cantCast` takes `from` too (Memory Vessel's lock). "Players
can't play lands" was already `prohibits.lands` (Null Chamber's) with `{}` —
only marked. The scaffolder reads the effect line and the three static
lines. Keys: effect:prohibit-land-plays, effect:prohibit-cast-by-zone.
Proving cards: Turf Wound, Pardic Miner, Territorial Dispute, Drannith
Magistrate, Experimental Frenzy. Left: Memory Vessel's "until your next
turn, players may play cards they exiled this way" (an impulse permission
ending as your next turn begins), Moonhold's mana-spent halves (built — a
`conditional` each), Rock Jockey's `condition:played-land-this-turn`.

### A limit on the spells a player casts each turn — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:cast-and-activate-restrictions`'s top
group, features only. A static `prohibits` takes `castLimit: N`: its `spells`
bind a player only once they've cast N spells matching them this turn, counted
per player from `spellsCastThisTurnAs`, each as it was cast — so the whole
turn counts, spells from before the permanent arrived and countered ones among
them, and a spell the filter doesn't match neither counts nor is barred (rules
101.2, 601.3). "Each player can't cast more than one spell each turn", its
"noncreature" and "non-Phyrexian" forms and Ethersworn Canonist's "who has cast
a nonartifact spell this turn" are the one shape. `who` takes
`"enchanted-player"`. A `player-effect`'s `castsLeft: { who?, count }` is the
one-shot, "you can cast only one more spell this turn" (the count so far plus
`count`, fixed as it resolves). Moderation and Yawgmoth's Agenda moved from a
`cast-this-turn` condition to `castLimit`. The scaffolder reads the lines and
"Add seven {R}". Keys: effect:cast-limit, effect:per-player-cast-limit. Proving
cards: Rule of Law, Deafening Silence, Ethersworn Canonist, Irencrag Feat,
Curse of Exhaustion. Left: Hedonist's Trove's "this way", Fires of Invention's
"no more than two" beside its "only during your turn" (one line, two statics).

### A tapped-for-mana trigger that uses the stack — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:tapped-for-mana-nonmana-trigger`, features
only. Rule 106.12a's moment is now an event, `permanent-tapped-for-mana` (the
object, the player who activated it, the types its own ability made), emitted by
a hand activation and by a payment's `useManaSource`, so the auto-payer sets it
off too. A `tapped-for-mana` trigger that `isTriggeredManaAbility` (adds mana, no
target; rule 605.1b) is still made with the mana by `tappedForManaExtras`; any
other goes on the stack the next time a player would get priority (603.3), its
trigger object the permanent, its `"trigger-player"` who tapped it. `pool.test`'s
guard narrows to the mana abilities. The scaffolder reads the trigger's wordings,
"~ deals N damage to that player", "it deals N damage to each opponent", "return
it to its owner's hand" and "target opponent creates … token". Key:
trigger:tapped-for-mana-nonmana-trigger. Proving cards: Manabarbs, Burning Earth,
Zhur-Taa Druid, Forbidden Orchard, Storm Cauldron, Sanctimony. Left: Treasure
Nabber (a gain-control "until the end of your next turn", and a sacrificed token
read by `matchesWho`), C.A.M.P. (fortify, and the colour of the mana produced,
which `produced` now carries).

### A card chosen from a set as an effect resolves — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:choose-card-exiled-with-source`'s group,
features only, beside `create-token-copy`'s `chooseOne`. `look-and-choose`'s
`zone: "exiled-with-source"` takes `destination: "stay"` (the card only chosen,
`then` acting on it: Lazav's `become-copy`), `under: "owners"` (Purgatory's
"return", rule 610.3c) and now reaches an "until this leaves" exile too;
`choose-exiled-to-play` takes `linked` (Muse Vessel); `discard-chosen` takes
`mayCast`, as `exile`'s (Psychic Intrusion). "From that player's graveyard or
hand" was already `discard-chosen`'s `alsoFrom`. The scaffolder reads all of it,
"you may pay {N} and N life. If you do" included, and links a "target player
exiles a card from their hand" on a face that reads "exiled with ~". Keys:
effect:choose-card-exiled-with-source, effect:choose-card-from-hand-or-graveyard.
Proving cards: Memory Leak, Psychic Intrusion, Muse Vessel, Purgatory, Bag of
Devouring, Lazav, Wearer of Faces. Left: Bane Alley Broker's "you may look at
cards exiled with this creature" past a change of control (`exiledFaceDown.lookers`
is fixed as it's exiled; and a face-down linked card picked is at random within
its group, `exileChoiceCandidates`), Vault 13's "the rest on the bottom" (the
linked branch of `beginZoneChoice` forces `leftover: "stay"`), Valki's mana-value
choice, and each one's other key.

### Revealing from a hand: the whole hand, at random, or up to N chosen — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:target-spec-additions`' top group, features
only. Nearly all of it was already there and the keys were stale: `reveal-hand`
(a whole hand, rule 701.20a), `choose-random-card`'s `zone: "hand"` with `show:
"reveal"` (`slot` beside the player, `then` on the card), and `look-and-choose`'s
`destination: "reveal"` with `{ thisWay: "revealed" }`. New: the amount's
`sharingManaValue` (Vault 21: House Gambit's count of the revealed cards sharing a
mana value with another), and the scaffolder, which now reads all of them:
"target player loses N life and reveals their hand", "… reveals their hand. You
gain life equal to the number of cards in that player's hand", "that player
reveals a card at random", "… at random … that card's mana value" as damage or a
gain, "reveal up to five …" with its count, and "you may reveal … If you do or if
you control another …" as a `conditional` with an `else`. Keys:
effect:reveal-cards-from-hand, effect:reveal-random-card-from-hand,
effect:target-player-reveals-hand. Proving cards: Thoughtcutter Agent, Search
Warrant, Merfolk Spy, Planeswalker's Fury, Armored Kincaller, Vault 21: House
Gambit. Left: Planeswalker's Favor's "+X/+X, the revealed card's mana value"
(its own key, amount:revealed-card-mana-value-keep-targets, though a `slot: 2`
may already say it).

### A cost reduction on the next spell — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:temporary-player-effect`'s top group,
features only. `grant-spells-this-turn` with `next` takes `reduceGeneric` (an
amount read as it resolves and fixed from then on) and `affinityFor` (a filter
counted as the spell is cast, rule 702.41a), read in `costModificationFor` (rule 601.2f) from every unspent grant
the spell would use up, so instances add up and all end on that one spell as it
becomes cast (601.2i), whatever it paid. "Until your next turn, instant and
sorcery spells you cast cost {1} less" was already a `player-effect`'s
`reduceSpells` with that duration — only tested and marked. The scaffolder reads
{N}, {X} "as this ability resolves" and affinity, and "whenever ~ attack" for a
plural name. Keys: effect:next-spell-cost-reduction,
effect:until-next-turn-spell-cost-reduction. Proving cards: Hardened Berserker,
Kaza, Roil Chaser, Maelstrom Muse, Don & Raph, Hard Science. Not tried: Panoptic
Projektor's "face-down creature spell" (a grant's filter on a spell cast face
down), which needs its other key anyway. Left: Will Kenrith's "that player casts" (its own key,
effect:player-effect-target-player).

### "When you discard this card", its cause, and the turn's first discard — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:discards-extensions`, features only.
A discard now records its cause: `cards-discarded` carries `cause`, the
controller of the spell or ability whose effect or cost discarded the cards
(`DecisionSource.controller`, kept on a queued discard's `source` and on an
`each-player-may`'s held discards), absent for the cleanup step's (rule
514.1). The new trigger `this-discarded` is "when you discard this card",
firing from wherever the discard put the card (rule 113.6k — the graveyard,
or exile for madness), this card's abilities alone; `byOpponent` is "when a
spell or ability an opponent controls causes you to discard this card", its
"that player" (`"trigger-player"`) the opponent. The `discards` trigger takes
`firstTimeEachTurn`, read off the turn's discard history (so a discard
before the permanent arrived was the first). "If an opponent discarded a
card this turn" (Tinybones) was already the `turn-history` `discarded`
condition — only marked. The scaffolder reads the three trigger lines. Keys:
trigger:discard-this-card, trigger:discards-extensions,
trigger:discards-first-time-each-turn. Proving cards: Psychic Purge,
Guerrilla Tactics, Metrognome, Bartered Cow, Titanbones, Towering Heart,
Rielle, the Everwise. Left: Toluz's "exile them" (the discarded cards,
linked — `effect:linked-exile-from-discard`), Inti's batch key
(`trigger:discards-batch-trigger`, likely only marking).

### Companion — built 2026-11-01

Rule 702.139, the need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:companion`. A definition's
`companion` is its condition as data (`companion.ts`: one `each-card` shape over a
scope and a test, plus `different-names`, `shared-card-type` and
`deck-size-over-minimum`), covering the ten printed ones, which the scaffolder reads
whole. Before the hands are drawn each player with an eligible card in their
sideboard is asked, starting player first, through a `choose-from-zone` flagged
`companion` (103.2b, no new decision kind; the reveal is public, and the other seats
don't see what's offered). `GameState.companions` holds the revealed card, and the
new `take-companion` action is the special action: {3}, sorcery timing, no stack
(116.2g). A Commander deck's `sideboard` keeps its companions alone (702.139d) and
`sideboardOf` gives it no sideboard to count or wish from (903.5e). In the client:
the deck builder's sideboard (which marks whether the deck fulfils the condition),
a "Companion" section in an imported list, and "companion" and "To hand" on the
player panel. Proving cards: Kaheera, Keruga. Left for `author-freed`: Lurrus's
castFromGraveyard needs its X held to the mana-value cap (the ruling), Zirda's
cost reduction beyond permanent sources and Jegantha's mana that can't pay generic
(both noted in their triage), Obosh's `filter:odd-mana-value`.

### A card's own copy limit — built 2026-11-01

"A deck can have any number of cards named ~" and "up to seven cards named ~":
the need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:deck-any-number-copies`. A definition's
`deckCopies` (`"any"` or a number) sets the card over the copy limit (rules
100.2a, 903.5b; the card wins, 101.1), and `copiesAllowed` in `deck-validation.ts`
is the one read of it, for the validator the server, the importer and the deck
builder share; a commander counts as one of its own copies. Nothing in play reads
it. The scaffolder reads both lines. Proving cards: Rat Colony, Relentless Rats,
Seven Dwarves. Left for `author-freed`: the other nine, two of them on another
need too (Slime Against Humanity, the Approaches).

### What blocked or was blocked this turn — built 2026-11-01

The top unit of the need-keys Phase 2 family
`static:combat-restriction-extensions`. Each declared block now goes into the
blocker's controller's `TurnHistory.blocks` (`BlockRecord`), both creatures as
they were then, so the new `CardFilter.blockedThisTurn` looks back past the end
of combat, a removal from combat and the other creature's leaving, which
`blockingRelation` can't: `relation` (`"blocked"`, `"blocked-by"`, `"either"`),
`partner` (a filter on the other creature as it was at the block — the
`asEntered` precedent, Mechan Shieldmate's ruling), `of` (a target slot,
`"each-target"` or `"source"`, bound to those objects and their stints) and
`thisCombat`. A `delayed-trigger` binds `of` as it's set up and keeps no targets,
so "destroy all creatures that blocked or were blocked by it" doesn't fizzle with
the target gone. New timing: `end-of-combat-this-turn` ("at this turn's next end
of combat"), which lapses with the turn. The scaffolder reads all of it. Keys:
filter:blocked-or-blocked-by-filter-this-turn, and rode along
filter:blocked-this-turn, filter:blocked-source-this-turn,
filter:blocked-by-source-this-combat. Proving cards: You Cannot Pass!, Sea Troll,
Venomous Breath, Ride Down, Triton Tactics, Sizzling Barrage, Joven's Ferrets.
Left for `author-freed` to narrow: Glyph of Reincarnation (returns a card per
creature destroyed), Glyph of Delusion (counters by the blocked creature's power),
Cathedral Membrane ("dies during combat"), and "attacked or blocked" cards
(Gideon's Triumph, Vizier of Deferment, Inferno Hellion, Lurker), which need an
`anyOf` with `attackedThisTurn` and their own shapes.

### Turning a face-down permanent face up by an effect — built 2026-11-01

The top unit of the need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:face-down`. The new
`turn-face-up` effect turns a face-down permanent up with nothing paid, however
it came to be face down (rule 708.7); it shares `Game.becomeFaceUp` with the
special action, so "when this is turned face up" and `asTurnedFaceUp` apply and
a megamorph's counter doesn't (702.37b). A permanent under `cant-turn-face-up`
is left alone, and an instant or sorcery card is revealed and stays face down
with nothing triggering (701.40g, 701.58g). "You may turn a face-down creature
you control face up" is `choose-permanents` over `faceDown`, no new decision.
The scaffolder reads each of those lines. Keys: effect:turn-face-up
(static:cant-turn-face-up was already built, and the effect now honours it).
Proving cards: Ixidor, Reality Sculptor, Break Open, Skirk Alarmist, Showstopping
Surprise, Karlov Watchdog. Left for `author-freed` to narrow: Etrata ("If you
can't, exile it, then you may cast"), Hauntwoods Shrieker (reveal target
face-down permanent, "if it's a creature card"), Kaust (`stat:turned-face-up-this-turn`),
Ugin's Mastery and Zimone (their triggers), Yarus, Expose the Culprit.

### Return all the cards put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family
`condition:return-cards-put-in-graveyard-from-battlefield-this-turn`, features
only: nothing new in the engine. `return-from-graveyard` with `count: "all"`
already read any filter, and the graveyard-history work below gave it
`putIntoGraveyardThisTurn`. What's new is the tests, the proving cards and the
scaffolder, which reads "return to the battlefield / to your hand all [types]
cards in your graveyard that were put there from the battlefield this turn",
"each player returns …" and "put onto the battlefield under your control all …
in all graveyards …", an "and" list of card types included. Proving cards:
Faith's Reward, Second Sunrise, Gleancrawler. Left for `author-freed` to narrow:
Supper for Spiders ("They are Food artifacts"), Lobelia ("X is the exiled
card's power"), Gerrard ("exile it and return …"), Brought Back (targets).

### A graveyard card put there this turn, by where it came from — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:filter-this-turn-history`, features
only. Every move into a graveyard now records the turn and the zone it came
from (`GameObject.putIntoGraveyardOn`, replacing the library-only turn
marker), read by the new `CardFilter.putIntoGraveyardThisTurn` (`{ from? }`):
"a creature card in a graveyard that was put there from the battlefield this
turn" (`from: ["battlefield"]`), "from anywhere this turn" (`{}`) — a card
still in that graveyard as the same object (rule 400.7). The older
`putIntoGraveyardFromLibraryThisTurn` reads the same record. "If a land you
controlled was put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn" was
already the `turn-history` `to-graveyard` condition with `controlledBy:
"you"` (the land as it last existed, 603.10a) — only marked. The scaffolder
reads "Choose target … card in … graveyard that was put there from … this
turn. Put that card onto the battlefield under your control / Return it to
your hand." and the land intervening "if". Keys:
filter:put-into-graveyard-from-battlefield-this-turn,
condition:land-put-into-graveyard-from-battlefield-this-turn,
condition:put-into-graveyard-this-turn-any-zone. Proving cards: Grim Return,
Wreck Hunter, Thrilling Encore, Night Nurse, Healer of Heroes, The Lady of
Otaria. Left: the family's fought-this-turn, blocked-by-this-source-this-combat
and lost-life-this-turn keys.

### Colours in addition to an object's own — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 group of `effect:add-subtype` (with
`effect:animate-add-colors`'s), features only: a colour added "in addition
to its other colors" (rule 105.3, layer 5, in timestamp order with the rest)
by `become-color`'s new `inAddition` (Indigo Faerie), `add-types`'s new
`addColors` (Unctus, Grand Metatect; a fresh timestamp so a later "becomes
black" overrides it) and `animate`'s (Phantasmal Form). The static form on
what it's attached to was there already (`StaticAbility.addColors`, Deep
Freeze) — only marked, and proved on Ghoulflesh. The scaffolder reads
Indigo Faerie's and Unctus's lines. Keys: effect:add-color-until-eot,
effect:animate-add-colors, static:add-color-to-attached. Proving cards:
Indigo Faerie, Unctus, Grand Metatect, Phantasmal Form, Ghoulflesh. Left:
Possessed Goat's "Activate only once" (not exhaust — no once-ever flag of
its own); Hunting Wilds needs `animate-all` colours over the lands put this
way, not an addition.

### Text-changing effects: one colour word or land type for another — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:text-change-color-or-land-words`,
features only. Rule 612's text change (layer 3, 613.1c) is a
`PtModifier.textChange`, written by the new `change-text` effect: both words
asked as it resolves (`choose-value`'s `"text-word"`, the existing
`choose-creature-type` menu the client already reads), for good or until end
of turn. `rulesTextName` names a changed definition the registry builds
(`text-change.ts`): every colour word in a filter, protection, a static's
scope, devotion or unspent mana, every basic land type and landwalk keyword,
Doom Blade's `"nonblack-creature"` shorthand, and the client text; its type
line's land types too, so a Forest made an Island taps for {U}. A spell
resolves by its changed text, re-checking its targets, and a permanent
spell keeps the change (400.7a). A pool audit pins where colours can sit.
The scaffolder reads the four shapes. Key:
effect:text-change-color-or-land-words. Proving cards: Sleight of Mind,
Magical Hack, Mind Bend, Crystal Spray. Left: a token the changed text
creates keeps its definition's colours (612.4); Swirl the Mists' static
changing every object's colour words; Sarpadian Empires' option words.

### The card revealed for a cost, and cards revealed that share a colour — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:reveal-card-as-additional-cost`, features
only, on the reveal-as-cost machinery (`BeholdSpec.fromHand`, 2026-10-30). A
cost's reveal from the hand — a spell's additional cost, a cost option, a
kicker reveal, an ability's `reveal` — is now remembered on the spell or
ability it paid for (`LastKnownRefs.revealed`, with what the card was in the
hand), read as the new `"revealed"` `AmountRef`: in the hand while it's
there, as it last existed there once it has left (the rulings). An
activated `reveal` takes `shareColor` (Illuminated Folio), offered only cards
with a colour enough of them have and asked with the choose-from-zone
decision's existing `together` rule "share", which the client reads already.
"Reveal a Pirate card from your hand or pay {2}" (Daring Buccaneer, Sadistic
Skymarcher, Surtland Elementalist) was already Lorwyn's cost option — only
marked. The scaffolder reads the mandatory reveal line, "Reveal two cards
from your hand that share a color" and the revealed card's -X/-X, damage,
exile-all, conditional exile and life. Keys:
cost:reveal-card-as-additional-cost, cost:reveal-cards-sharing-color.
Proving cards: Induce Despair, Charge of the Forever-Beast, Calamity of the
Titans, Titan's Presence, Living Destiny, Illuminated Folio. Left: a copy
of such a spell reads no revealed card (as a copy's sacrificed and discarded
don't — rule 707.10).

### Sacrifice costs matched against the source — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:sacrifice-multiple`'s first group, features
only. A sacrifice cost's filters — a `{ filter }`, a `{ filter, count }` and
each of an `each`'s — were matched with no source, so every clause about it
(`isSource`, `hostOfSource`, `ofChosenType`) matched nothing. They are now
matched against the ability's source, in the legal-action offer, the
activation check, the mana plan that keeps the victims for last and the
`pendingCostSacrifice` parts as they're asked. "Sacrifice this creature and
another green creature" is an `each` with an `{ isSource: true }` part;
"another creature or an artifact" an `anyOf` with `isSource: false` on one
branch (Skophos Warleader, Slaughter-Priest of Mogis and Skullport Merchant,
authored with `otherOnly` across both branches, moved to it); "Sacrifice
enchanted creature" `hostOfSource`; "a creature of the chosen type"
`ofChosenType`. The scaffolder reads all four. Keys:
cost:sacrifice-self-and-another, filter:not-source-clause,
cost:sacrifice-enchanted-creature, cost:chosen-type-sacrifice-cost. Proving
cards: Mold Folk, Malevolent Noble, Doom Cannon, Etchings of the Chosen,
Betrothed of Fire, Bloodfire Infusion, Magus of the Order, Great Hall of
Starnheim, Sandstone Deadfall. Left: Mount Doom (each player chooses, destroy
the rest), Emrakul's Evangel ("any number of other"), Kyscu Drake's "a
creature named Spitting Drake" in the scaffolder (the engine runs it).

### A card name chosen with a player, the player's draws revealed — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:name-a-card`, features only. The
decision itself was B246's `choose-card-name` (resolving) and
`chooseCardNameOnEnter`: Cabal Therapy and Predict were already expressible
and are only proved (the scaffolder now reads both, and a sacrifice-only
"Flashback—Sacrifice …" line). New: the static **`revealsDraws:
"chosen-player"`** ("the chosen player reveals each card they draw", rule
701.20a), and a `draws` trigger's `filter` reading the permanent, so
`{ chosenName: true }` is "a card with the chosen name"; the scaffolder reads
Booby Trap whole (its "choose an opponent and a card name", the reveal, the
trigger, "sacrifice ~. If you do, ~ deals N damage to that player"). Keys:
decision:name-a-card, trigger:chosen-player-draws-named-card. The cards the
key held each need something else as well (Liar's Pendulum's guess, Spy Kit's
every name, Conundrum and Petra Sphinx's per-player reveal-and-sort, The
Clone Saga's chosen-name delayed trigger, Demonic Consultation's and Spoils of
the Vault's reveal-until a name): `author-freed` narrows them. Proving cards:
Booby Trap, Cabal Therapy, Predict.

### One choice across zones, or one card a slot — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-from-zone-extensions`, features
only, on the `choose-from-zone` decision the client already reads (it captions
each card's zone when they differ, and gates Confirm on a set rule).
`look-and-choose` takes a search's **`together`** — `oneEach` slots, a card
filling any slot it matches, one card a slot, with a `text` for the prompt
(Atraxa, Grand Unifier's card types, Harper Recruiter's four creature types);
since 2026-11-01 also **`oneEachTypeAmong`** (the types among noncreature spells
cast this turn, Hurkyl, Master Wizard), **`totalManaValueAtMost`** (Smelting
Vat), **`split`** for a destination by what each chosen card is (Zimone's
Experiment), **`from`** another player's graveyard (Grime Gorger), and the
discarded cards a replacement exiled (Queen Kayla bin-Kroog). A chooser other
than the controller: `search-library`'s **`instead.chooser`** and `pick: {
exactly }` (Intuition, Gifts Ungiven, Realms Uncharted), `look-and-choose`'s
**`chooser: "an-opponent"`** (Guided Passage) and **`under: "you"`** (Scrounge),
`for-each-player`'s **`choicesHeld`** (Dredge the Mire, Rejoin the Fight),
`discard-chosen`'s **`chooser`** (Rakdos Augermage).
`discard-chosen` takes **`alsoFrom`**: the graveyard beside the revealed hand,
by its own filter (Agonizing Remorse), or the library's revealed top card
(Psychotic Episode, with a new `destination: "library-bottom"`).
`return-from-graveyard`'s **`count`** is an amount (Worldsoul's Rage's "up to
X … from your hand and/or graveyard"). The scaffolder reads all five cards'
lines whole. Keys: decision:choose-from-zone-extensions (its 2026-10-27
description — a pick across hand and graveyard, a per-card-type pick),
decision:choose-from-hand-and-graveyard,
decision:choose-from-hand-and-library-top, effect:look-and-choose-one-each.
Still open beside them: Selective Adaptation (a keyword slot each — whether
one card may be chosen for two keywords has no ruling — and "put one of the
chosen cards onto the battlefield"), Portent of Calamity's "four or more cards
this way", Final Parting's split to hand and graveyard (a split search's
prompt doesn't say which pick goes where), and the other keys of the family.
Proving cards: Atraxa, Grand Unifier; Harper Recruiter; Agonizing Remorse;
Psychotic Episode; Worldsoul's Rage.

### What's attached to a target or a player — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:filter-attachments`' first group
(and `filter:attached-to-target` beside it, the same code), features only, on
the target-relations family's. `CardFilter.attachedToTarget: n` (an effect's
filter, bound by `bindDynamicCompares` to `attachedToRef`, the slot's object
or player as the effect applies; an illegal slot matches nothing — rule
608.2b) — Hubris, Blastfire Bolt, Strip Bare, Eaten by Spiders, Street
Sweeper, Portal of Sanctuary, Word of Undoing, Orzhov Charm, Essence
Reliquary, Scarab of the Unseen; `CardFilter.attachedToPlayer: "you" |
"opponent"` (an Aura on a player, rule 303.4 — Witchbane Orb); and the
`other` slot relation `attachedToThan` ("up to one target Equipment attached
to that creature" — Fiery Annihilation), narrowed through
`TargetFacts.attachedTo`, refused when broken (601.2c) and judged again in
`targetLegality`: no longer attached, or the creature illegal, the Equipment
slot is illegal (the rulings). The scaffolder reads "(destroy|exile|return)
target … and all Auras/Equipment attached to it/that creature" as one
simultaneous instruction (the attachments first), "destroy/exile all
Auras/Equipment/Auras and Equipment/Curses attached to that creature / target
… / you", "exile up to one target Equipment attached to that creature", and
Lava Coil's "If that creature would die this turn, exile it instead" past
such a slot. Keys: condition:attached-to-target, filter:attached-to-player,
filter:attached-to-target. Proving cards: Hubris, Blastfire Bolt, Witchbane
Orb, Fiery Annihilation. Left for `author-freed` to narrow: Light of
Judgment ("destroy up to one Equipment attached to that creature" — a choice
as it resolves), Awaken the Sleeper ("if it's equipped, you may …"), Glamer
Spinners and Simic Guildmage (moving an Aura to another permanent), Fulgent
Distraction (unattach).

### Countering abilities: their sources, and every one on the stack — built 2026-11-02

Family `effect:counter-ability`. Counter a target ability was already there
(Stifle, Azorius Guildmage), and `stack` already held Disallow's three nouns.
New: `counter`'s `thenSource` (an effect on the ability's source, while it's
still that permanent); `for-each-on-stack`, a combinator over the stack like
`for-each-permanent`, never what's resolving (rule 608.2n); `ability.whose:
"opponent"` and `stack.whose` / `stack.targeting`. Keys: effect:counter-ability,
effect:counter-all-abilities. Found to need no code: Disallow. Proving cards:
Disallow, Green Slime, Ouphe Vandals, Nimble Obstructionist, Tishana's Tidebinder,
Teferi's Response, Kadena's Silencer, Summary Dismissal. Left open: Strict Proctor,
whose trigger is `trigger:ability-triggered-by-entering`.

### Any number with different names or powers — built 2026-11-02

Family `decision:choose-from-zone-extensions`, its top unit. `together` gains
`distinctPowers` beside `distinctNames`, on a search and a `look-and-choose`,
and `choose-permanents` takes either: the awaiting and the offer carry the
same `ZoneChoiceTogether`, so a bot, the fuzzer and the client read one rule.
No new decision. Names are `namesOf`, so a face-down permanent has none and
can't be chosen (rule 201.2b). A `search-library`'s `instead.destination:
"library"` is "shuffle the chosen cards into your library". The client's picks,
on the board and in the popup, swap out whichever they can't sit beside. Keys:
decision:choose-any-number-different-names,
decision:choose-any-number-different-powers. Proving cards: Eerie Ultimatum,
Battle for Bretagard, Sigarda's Vanguard, Rip, Spawn Hunter, Threats Undetected.

### Relations among the chosen targets: shared types, equal toughness, different names — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:target-spec-additions`'s first group,
features only, beside `sameController`. An `other` slot's **`shares`** (a
`TargetShare`: card types or creature types, `among` the card types that
count, `none` for "share no creature types") — Shifting Loyalties, Legerdemain,
Rivals' Duel, Secret Tunnel, Unbury; an `any-number` group's
**`equalToughness`** (V.A.T.S.) and **`differentNames`** (Behold the Sinister
Six!). Each is narrowed in `slotOptions`, refused by `invalidTargetReason`
(rule 601.2c) and judged again in `targetLegality` (608.2b): a pair no longer
sharing are both illegal, one already illegal read as it last existed
(`targetZones` now reaches `targetLegality`); a group no longer keeping its
relation, over the members still legal, makes every member illegal (V.A.T.S.'s
ruling). `TargetFacts` gained `toughnessOf`, `nameOf` and `shareTypes`
(`targeting.ts`'s `typesShare`). The scaffolder reads "two target … that share
a card type / a creature type / no creature types [from your graveyard]",
"any number of / up to N target … with equal toughness / different names",
"exchange control of two target … that share a card type", "… and (another)
target … that shares one of those types / a card type with it", the pair
"can't be blocked this turn", and "Choose … . Those creatures fight each
other. / Destroy the chosen creatures.". Keys: effect:targets-share-type,
effect:targets-equal-toughness, effect:targets-with-different-names. Proving
cards: Legerdemain, Rivals' Duel, Secret Tunnel, V.A.T.S., Behold the Sinister
Six!. Not this feature after all: Mind Extraction (the sacrificed creature's
colours) and Dead Ringers ("unless either one is a color the other isn't").

### A graveyard's matching cards, or target cards, shuffled into libraries — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:shuffle-library`, features only, on the
library-ordering family's code. `shuffle-graveyard-into-library` takes a
**`filter`** (only the graveyard's matching cards, the library shuffled either
way — Barishi, Elixir, Repopulate); **`put-on-libraries`' `position:
"shuffle"`** puts target cards into their owners' libraries at once and
shuffles each library a card went into (Turn the Earth), `withSource` sending
the resolving spell too (Serene Remembrance). Both log a new
`cards-shuffled-into-library` event, read by a new `"shuffled-into-library"`
this-way kind (Elixir's life). "Target player shuffles up to N target cards
from their graveyard into their library" was already Witness the Future's
composition (Memory's Journey, Stream of Consciousness, Krosan Reclamation),
and "shuffle any number of target cards from your graveyard into your library"
Piper's Melody's (Perpetual Timepiece, The Bath Song) — only marked. The
scaffolder reads "shuffle all … cards from your / target player's graveyard
into …", Elixir's life line and Serene Remembrance's line. Keys:
effect:shuffle-graveyard-filter-into-library,
effect:shuffle-graveyard-targets-into-library. Proving cards: Elixir,
Repopulate, Barishi, Turn the Earth, Serene Remembrance, Perpetual Timepiece.

### Several cards into libraries at once, their owners ordering them — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:library-ordering`, features only. New
effect **`put-on-libraries { what: { targetsFrom } | { all }, position:
"top" | "bottom" | "owner-chooses" }`**: several cards put into their owners'
libraries as one instruction (one leave event), then each owner, APNAP (rule
101.4), arranging their own on each end (rule 401.4) through the existing
`choose-from-zone` order (`Game.beginLibraryOrder`, scry's), asked only when
the order is a choice; `"owner-chooses"` asks each owner first which of their
permanents go on the bottom (an existing `choose-permanents`, `keepChoice`),
then moves everything at once. No new decision kind. Bone Harvest, Footbottom
Feast and Frantic Salvage moved onto it (they had been moving one at a time in
target order). "Look at target player's library and reorder" was already
`look-and-choose`'s `from` with `top-any-order` (Cruel Fate); `chooser:
"that-player"` now also covers a library (Tahngarth's Glare's second look).
The scaffolder reads "put two/any number of/up to N target … on top of their
owners' libraries / your library", "put all … on top of their owners'
libraries" and Aetherspouts' line. Keys: decision:owner-orders-library-placements,
effect:look-at-target-player-library-reorder, effect:put-on-top-of-library-all.
Proving cards: Plow Under, Forever Young, Harmonic Convergence, Aetherspouts,
Ransack, Dimir Machinations, Tahngarth's Glare. Left: several cards "Nth from
the top" at once (Riptide Gearhulk — `effect:put-into-library-nth-from-top-together`).

### Prevention divided among targets, and the next N damage to you — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:prevent-next-n-damage-to-you` with
`effect:prevention-divided`, features only. New effect
**`prevent-damage-divided { from, combatOnly? }`**: "prevent the next N damage
that would be dealt this turn to any number of targets, divided as you choose"
— the division announced as it's cast or activated, at least 1 each (601.2d),
each share a `prevent-damage` shield on its target (615.7), a target gone
illegal getting none (608.2b); the client's division prompt reads "damage to
prevent". "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to you this turn" was
already B250's `damage-rule` with `to: { players: "you" }` and an amount
(Festival of the Guildpact), only marked. The scaffolder reads both, and
"… combat damage …" as `combat: true`. Keys: effect:prevent-next-n-damage-to-you,
effect:prevention-divided. Proving cards: Remedy, Embolden, Conservator,
Shield of the Ages, Decorated Griffin.

### A restriction over a target player's creatures, or the ones there now — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:combat-restriction-extensions`, its top
group, features only. `restrict`'s `filter` form (a turn-wide rule, rule
611.2c) takes **`controlledByTarget`** (read from the side of the player in a
target slot, or the controller of the permanent there — "creatures target
player controls can't attack this turn", "… controlled by that player or by
that planeswalker's controller") and **`exceptTarget`** ("other creatures that
player controls", the rule's `except` held by stint, 400.7 —
`turnRuleCovers`), and **`asItResolves`**: the restrictions as modifiers on
what matches now, where the same sentence's bonus fixes the set ("each creature
you control gets +3/+3 … and must be blocked this turn if able"). "Can't be
blocked this turn" as a rule over a name or every creature was already
`restrictions: ["cant-be-blocked"]`, only marked. The scaffolder reads
"creatures target player controls / you control / your opponents control /
[every creature] can't attack / block / be blocked this turn". Keys:
effect:cant-be-blocked-rule-this-turn, effect:restrict-controlled-by-target,
effect:restrict-each-matching-at-resolution, effect:restrict-rule-for-player.
Proving cards: Concussive Bolt, Mark for Death, Joraga Invocation, Suicidal
Charge, Leitmotif Composer, Veiling Oddity.

### "Can't be the target of …" statics — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `keyword:hexproof-from-colors`, its one group,
features only. The printed restrictions that aren't the keyword (rules 601.2c,
602.2b) are `hexproofFrom` with qualifiers: **`spellsOnly`** ("… of black or red
spells your opponents control"), **`abilitiesOnly`** ("… of abilities from
artifact sources"), **`anyController`** (no "your opponents control": its own
controller's too), and no colour, type or filter for every spell or ability
("can't be the targets of spells"). A qualified instance is kept apart from the
keyword's union (`Characteristics.hexproofFrom.qualified`) and judged alone in
`hexproofFromBlocks`, which tells a spell's targeting from an ability's by
`TargetSource.spell` (set by `cardSource`). New static
**`graveyardCardsUntargetable: { filter?, byOpponents? }`** — "cards in
graveyards can't be the targets of spells or abilities" — read by
`isLegalTarget` (`graveyardCardShielded`). "Hexproof from multicolored" was
already `hexproofFrom: { filter: { multicolored: true } }`. The scaffolder reads
"~ / Enchanted creature / Creatures [you control] can't be the target(s) of …"
and the graveyard line. Keys: static:cant-be-targeted-by-filter,
keyword:hexproof-from-multicolored, static:hexproof-from-spells-only,
static:untargetable-by-color-any-player. Proving cards: Fiendslayer Paladin,
Mercenary Informer, Dense Foliage, Ground Seal, Silent Gravestone. Still open:
a one-shot "… can't be the targets of blue or black spells your opponents
control this turn" over permanents you control (Display of Dominance — a
turn-wide rule, not a characteristic), "unless it attacked or blocked this
turn" (Lurker), the Flagbearers' targeting requirement.

### Combat damage as though unblocked — built 2026-10-30

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:assign-damage-as-though-unblocked`,
features only. **`assignAsThoughUnblocked: true`** on a static is "you may
have [it] assign its combat damage as though it weren't blocked" — on
`"self"` (Thorn Elemental), the enchanted creature (Indomitable Might) or a
filter under a condition (Siege Behemoth): a rule about how it assigns
damage, so no loss of abilities ends it. The one-shot form is the
**`assign-as-though-unblocked`** effect (`target`, or `filter` as a rule for
the turn — Predatory Focus — and `optional`; without it there's no choice:
Outmaneuver). `unblockedAssignmentOf` reads it, "must" over "may". A blocked
attacker that may is always a question on the `assign-combat-damage`
decision (`mayUnblocked`; the answer's `asThoughUnblocked` sends all of it to
what it's attacking, rule 510.1b), even with one blocker or none left; under
banding the choice comes first, alone (`unblockedOnly`), and the other side
divides if declined. A blocked creature with no blocker left and no trample
assigns nothing, now a legal answer (510.1c). The client's damage bar has
"All to [defender]". New filter **`blocked`** (Outmaneuver's "target blocked
creatures"). The bots' `damageThrough` lets such an attacker's damage past
blocks. Key: assign-damage-as-though-unblocked. Proving cards: Thorn
Elemental, Indomitable Might, Siege Behemoth, Outmaneuver, Predatory Focus.
`static:attack-as-though-haste` (2 cards) is separate code and still open.

### A divided total read as it's announced, and a reflexive trigger that divides — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:divide-among-targets`, its top group,
features only. A **`divided.total` is any `EffectAmount`** (a spell's, an
activated ability's, a triggered ability's), read when the division is
announced — as the spell is cast, the ability activated, the trigger put on
the stack (rules 601.2d, 602.2b, 603.3d) — and fixed from then on
(`Game.dividedNow`, `triggerDivided` with the trigger's last-known refs); the
offer carries the number read and caps the group at one target per point
(`specsCappedByDivision`). A **reflexive trigger can carry `divided`**
(`ReflexiveTrigger.divided`, the `reflexive-trigger` effect's), its targets
and split announced as it's put on the stack (603.12). The scaffolder reads
"~/it deals N / X / damage equal to its power divided as you choose among
…, where X is …". Keys: divided-dynamic-total, reflexive-divided-damage.
Proving cards: Volcanic Wind, Monstrous Onslaught, Lukka, Bound to Ruin,
Ureni, the Song Unending, Orca, Siege Demon, Death to Our Enemies. Still
open: an X-reading total beyond a plain X (Meteor Shower's "X plus 1",
Shatterskull Smashing's "twice X if X is 6 or more") — the offer names a
number or `x`, so the client would need the amount; the scaffolder doesn't
read "When you do" at all.

### Attack triggers narrowed by attackers and the player attacked — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `trigger:attacks-player-filter`, features only. `attacks-player`'s
**`filter`** narrows the attackers that count: still once per player attacked by at least one of
them, and `{ triggerValue: true }` counts only them. `attacks`' **`attackedPlayer`** (a `TriggerWho`,
`"attached"` for a Curse's "enchanted player") is once per creature and never a creature attacking
that player's planeswalker (rule 508.3a). The scaffolder reads "whenever one or more … you control
attack a player" and "whenever a creature attacks enchanted player". Keys: attacks-player-filter,
creature-attacks-enchanted-player. Proving cards: Attuma, Atlantean Warlord, Curse of Predation,
Curse of the Forsaken. Freed but not authored: Crimson Cowl, Whirlwind, Bitter Work, Meriadoc
Brandybuck, Dollmaker's Shop, Canal Courier (its "attack different players" isn't this trigger, so
`author-freed` should give it a narrow key).

### A reflexive ability that names what its parent made or targeted — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:reflexive-trigger`, its top group, features only. A
`reflexive-trigger`'s **`about`** (`"created"`, `"put-this-way"` or a slot of the creating ability)
is read as it triggers and becomes the reflexive ability's trigger object, followed from that stint
(rules 603.12, 400.7, 608.2h), so everything that reads `"trigger-object"` works in its effect, an
`attachedTo` included. Naming nothing, nothing triggers. A bot reads an `about` slot by what the
reflexive ability does to it (`target-polarity.ts`). The scaffolder reads three clauses after a
step it read whole (`reflexiveAboutAt`): "attach it to target creature you control", "it/that
creature fights up to one target creature you don't control", and "return up to one target Aura or
Equipment card … attached to that token". Keys: reflexive-refers-to-creating-objects,
reflexive-refers-to-parent-target, reflexive-trigger-outer-target. Proving cards: Dáin Ironfoot,
Back for More, Curse of the Werefox, Forum Filibuster, Behemoth of Vault 0. Freed but not authored:
Thorin, Mountain-king ("become attached this way" needs its own count of attachments), Hans
Eriksson, Jailbreak, Ratonhnhaké꞉ton (`author-freed` re-reads each).

### The aggregate amount over any set — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:amount-aggregate`, its top group with
Tier B, features only. One **`AggregateSpec`** read through
`aggregateMatches`: `in` the battlefield, `"graveyards"`, `"exile"` or
`"attackers"` (the ones that counted for the `attacks-batch`/`attack-with`
trigger that fired — `LastKnownRefs.attackedTogether`, read as it resolves or
as last on the battlefield, rule 608.2h), a list of zones, `plus` sets with
filters of their own; `aggregate: "count"`; `of: "counters"`, every counter of
every kind; an effect amount's `forTarget`. **`grantPtPerCount.aggregate`**
(Carrion Grub, Emissary Escort); `thisWay`'s **`greatestOf`** (Heretic's
Punishment). Keys: amount-aggregate, aggregate-over-graveyard,
aggregate-of-target-players-permanents, amount-graveyard-total-mana-value,
attack-batch-aggregates, count-in-exile, aggregate-all-counters,
aggregate-all-counters-for-target, greatest-mana-value-this-way. Proving cards:
Carrion Grub, Emissary Escort, Ambitious Dragonborn, Crackling Drake, Maester
Seymour, Ferrafor, Young Yew, Shriekwood Devourer, Vulpine Harvester, Heretic's
Punishment. Still open: a batch of cards that left a graveyard (Amzu), a static
condition counting its own permanent (Backstreet Bruiser), the per-player
greatest discard (Scythe Specter, Cait), the
power of creatures that died together or this turn.

### "Has all activated and triggered abilities of the exiled card" — built 2026-11-03

The rest of `static:gain-abilities-from-graveyard`. `grantsActivatedOf`'s new

### "Has all activated abilities of …" — built 2026-10-31

The need-keys Phase 2 family `static:gain-abilities-from-graveyard`, its top

in rule 613.8's dependency order, a loop by timestamp; abilities that work only
