cost exiled; last-known power of moved cards) need code of their own.

### A card in exile, and the card exiled with this — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:linked-exiled-card`'s top group:
`effect:linked-exiled-card`, `effect:linked-face-down-exile-to-battlefield`,
`target:card-in-exile`, and `condition:filter-has-flashback` riding along. The
target spec `card-in-exile` (`whose` its owner, `faceUp`, `linked` — rule
607.2a, the source's stint, exiled with it or until it leaves — and a filter a
face-down card never matches, 406.3a); the filter `hasFlashback`;
`allow-cast-linked-exile`'s `spendAs` (rule 609.4b). The rest was already
there: `turn-exiled-face-up` then `return-exiled-by-source { linked:
"battlefield", under: "you", filter }` is "turn the exiled card face up. If it's
a creature card, put it onto the battlefield", and a `trigger-object` condition
over `sharesCardTypeWith: "exiled-with-source"` is "if it shares a card type
with the exiled card". `linked-exiled-card.test.ts`. Proving cards: Pull from
Eternity, Gelatinous Cube, Runic Repetition, Clone Shell, Cemetery Protector,
King Narfi's Betrayal. Still blocked under these keys: Summoner's Egg (a card
exiled from hand face down, `effect:exile-from-hand-face-down-linked`),
Rootcoil Creeper (mana spendable only on spells cast from a graveyard), Altar
of the Lost; the family's one-card units (Diabolic Servitude, Dark Impostor,
Hedonist's Trove) are left.

### A secret choice — built 2026-11-03

The need-keys Phase 2 key `decision:secret-choice` (no new decision kind). A
player chosen secretly as a permanent enters (`secretChoiceOnEnter`) or as an
ability resolves (`choose-opponent { remember, secret }`), or a word from a short
list (`choose-creature-type { options, remember, secret }`), kept as
`GameObject.secretChoice`: `viewFor` leaves the answer out of every view but the
chooser's and the log has only `secret-choice-made`, until the cost `revealChosen`
(payable by the chooser alone) shows it with `secret-choice-revealed`. With it:
`onlyOnce` for a plain "Activate only once", which moves seven cards off `exhaust`;
`controlledBy: "chosen-player"`; `stackObjectTargets`, a filter's `targets` that
reads an ability too; a player's protection from a player
(`player-effect { protectionFromPlayers }`, rule 702.16k) and a grant's
`fromChosenPlayer`. Key also marked: `effect:protection-from-player-for-permanent`.
`secret-choice.test.ts` (engine and server, whose test reads real frames). Proving
cards: Stalking Leonin, Guardian Archon, Emissary of Grudges, A Killer Among Us,
Goblin Game. Two of the key's cards are still blocked: The Toymaker's Trap (a
number not chosen before, then an opponent's guess) and Stronghold Gambit (each
player secretly picks a card in hand, then the lowest creature enters).

### The chosen player — built 2026-10-29

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-player` (features only). A
player chosen as a permanent enters (`choosePlayerOnEnter: "player" |
"opponent"`, asked through the existing `choose-creature-type` decision with
players as options — no new decision kind) or by an ability, kept
(`choose-opponent { remember }`), on `GameObject.chosenPlayer`; read back by
the `"chosen-player"` `PlayerScope`, the `step-begins` subject `who:
"chosen-player"`, `maxHandSize { who: "chosen-player" }` and the
`attackingChosenPlayer` filter. `choose-opponent` also gained a non-random
`anyPlayer` and `mostLife`. The client flags the chosen player on the tile.
Proving cards: Black Vise, The Rack, Cursed Rack, Saskia the Unyielding,
Triarch Stalker, Beckoning Will-o'-Wisp, The Master, Gallifrey's End. Keys:
`decision:choose-player`, `effect:choose-opponent-most-life`,
`static:attacking-chosen-player-anthem`, `trigger:chosen-player-upkeep`. Not
covered: two chosen players (Bitter Feud, Sower of Discord), a secret choice,
mana added by a chosen player (Valleymaker, Victory Chimes, Stadium
Vendors), "another target creature that player controls" (Strax), counts
over the chosen player's permanents (`amount:count-for-chosen-player`, the
next family), "deals 1 damage to itself" (Stuffy Doll) — `author-freed`
narrows them.

### Separating into piles — built 2026-10-29

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:separate-into-piles` (features only).
Rule 700.3's piles as the **`separate-into-piles`** effect: the objects
(`libraryTop` revealed or looked at, a player's `permanents`, your
`graveyard`'s matching cards, the spell's `"targets"`), a `separator` and a
`chooser` (`EffectPlayerRef`; "an opponent" is a `choose-opponent` around it),
`faceDown` for "a face-down pile and a face-up pile", and each pile's
`PileFate` (a zone, or an effect per object as target 0, one event). Two new
decision kinds, **`separate-piles`** and **`choose-pile`** (29th and 30th),
answered in the client by a pile popup (cards) or on the board (permanents).
Proving cards: Fact or Fiction, Curator of Destinies, Liliana of the Veil,
Death or Glory. Key: `decision:separate-into-piles`. Not covered: three
piles chosen at random (Whims of the Fates), "on the bottom in any order"
(Jace, Architect of Thought; Truth or Tale), play from a pile (Brilliant
Ultimatum), search a pile (Phyrexian Portal), each player's own lands
(Bend or Break), combat piles (Camouflage, Raging River) — `author-freed`
narrows them.

### Damage by this source, switches, the defending player's restrictions, other players' choices — built 2026-10-29

Five need-keys Phase 2 families, features only (`build-family` since
2026-10-29: the cards they free wait for `author-freed`). **`damagedThisTurnBy:
{ source: "this" | "host" }`**, matched by the source's battlefield stint
(`DamageSourceRecord.sourceZoneChangeCount`, 400.7, 603.10a), in a dies
trigger's filter and a "would die, exile it instead" replacement's (which now
gets its source). **`switch-pt`**: `PtModifier.switchPt`, layer 7d (613.4d).
**`cantAttackUnlessDefender` / `cantAttackIfDefender` /
`cantBlockUnlessAttacker`**: a `PlayerTest` asked of the player an attack or
block involves (506.2, 508.1c, 509.1b). **A chooser a target slot names**
(`ChosenPlayerRef`) for `for-each-player` and a `vote`'s `choose`.
**`discard-chosen`'s `destination`** (exile, linked by default; top of
library; the battlefield under the chooser) and a `target` the event names.
**`TriggeredAbility.targetsChosenBy`**: another player answers the
`choose-targets` decision (`targetsFor` its controller — 603.3d, 700.2e). Keys:
`trigger:dies-after-dealt-damage-by-source`,
`filter:damaged-this-turn-by-source`, `effect:switch-pt`,
`static:attack-restriction-defending-player-condition`,
`effect:target-player-exiles-from-hand`,
`effect:player-exiles-chosen-from-hand`, `effect:target-opponent-chooses`,
`effect:each-opponent-exiles-choice`,
`effect:target-player-hand-card-to-library-top`,
`effect:exile-chosen-from-revealed-hand`,
`effect:put-from-opponent-hand-onto-battlefield`,
`target:chosen-by-other-player`,
`decision:trigger-object-controller-chooses-target`.

### Counters removed by choice, modal activated abilities, an Aura's lock, dynamic counts — built 2026-10-29

Four need-keys Phase 2 families in one build (B254). **The `remove-counters`
decision** (the 28th kind): counters chosen from objects between a min and a
max — a cost paid once the ability is on the stack (`AbilityCost.removeCounter`'s
`kind: "any"`, `orKinds`, `from`, `excludeSource`, `among`, `orSuspended`;
602.2b, 601.2h, 118.3) or the `remove-counters` effect (`from` / `target`,
`count` or `"any"`, `upTo`, `perObject`), read back as `thisWay:
"counters-removed"`. **Modal activated abilities**: an announced `modal`
chosen as it's activated (700.2b), offered once per mode set with its modes'
targets (the `modes` of the offer and action). **`hostOfSource`** with
`prohibits.onlyTapAbilities` (Arrest, Serra Bestiary) and `counter`'s
`lockSource` (Trickbind). **Amounts in fixed fields**: `countOf`'s
`forEachPlayer` with a scoped `sacrifice` read per player, `half`'s
`fraction`, `put-on-library`'s `beneathTop`, `manifest`'s `count`, `may`'s
`maxX`. Keys: `cost:remove-counters-from-chosen`,
`cost:remove-counter-from-other-permanent-or-suspended-card`,
`cost:remove-counter-any-kind`, `effect:remove-counters-up-to`,
`effect:modal-activated-targeted-modes`, `effect:modal-activated-ability`,
`static:attached-activated-abilities-prohibited`,
`static:prohibit-tap-abilities-of-attached`,
`effect:prohibit-source-of-countered-ability`, `effect:sacrifice-amount`,
`effect:put-on-library-dynamic-position`, `effect:manifest-count-amount`,
`effect:may-pay-x-capped`.

### Granted statics, land-type setting, exchange of control — built 2026-10-29

Four need-keys Phase 2 families in one build (B253). **`grant-static`**: a
static ability on a permanent's modifier, read by the layer fold's
contributing statics (timestamped 613.7a), the block filters, the ability
grants, the untap step's and game-loss statics, and a layer-7b set for a
granted "base power is …" (not a CDA, 604.3a); **`restrict`'s
`cantBeBlockedBy`** ("can't be blocked by [X] this turn", a rule rather than
an ability) and **`CardFilter.not`**. **Rule 305.7**: a land set to a basic
land type loses its rules-text abilities (`landTextLost`, a loss "before every
grant"), keeps granted ones and gains the intrinsic mana ability; a land's own
layer-4 static depends on the setter (613.8a — `withoutTextLostSources`), and
it enters with none of its own entering abilities (614.12);
`setsChosenLandType`, and `choose-creature-type`'s fixed `options` for "the
basic land type of your choice". **`exchange-control`** (701.12a/b) and
`exchange-control-all`; `gain-control-all`'s `controlledByTarget`. Keys:
`effect:grant-static-to-permanent`, `effect:cant-be-blocked-by-filter-until-eot`,
`static:land-type-setting`, `static:choose-basic-land-type`,
`decision:choose-land-type-on-resolution`, `effect:set-land-type`,
`effect:become-basic-land-type`, `effect:exchange-control`,
`effect:exchange-control-all-with-target-opponent`,
`effect:gain-control-all-target-player`.

### Each player chooses, shared-graveyard targets, repeat this process — built 2026-10-28

Three need-keys Phase 2 families in one build (B251). **Rule 101.4 beyond
`each-player-may`**: a scoped `discard` holds every chosen card face down in
hand until all players have chosen, then discards them at once (Stronghold
Rats, Smallpox); a card chosen for an `each-player-may`'s `putFromHand` waits
the same way and they all enter together (Show and Tell). **"Each player
chooses"** is a `vote` with `choose: true` — no additional votes, no voting
triggers, `who`, `startingWith`, a creature type (`for: { creatureType }`), a
permanent read from the chooser's side (`own`) — and `with-choices`
substitutes `CHOSEN_THIS_WAY` (Harsh Mercy, Eye of Doom, Master of
Ceremonies). **`sameController`** on an `other` slot or an `any-number`
group: "from a single graveyard", "from an opponent's graveyard", "from
their graveyard" (`{ slot }`), "that player controls", rechecked as it
resolves (608.2b). **`repeat`**: `times`, `again` (a condition over the pass
just done — each pass its own "this way", `ctx.scopeThisWay`) and
`mayAgain`; the `this-way` condition's `sharing`. Keys:
`effect:hidden-simultaneous-choices`, `effect:each-player-chooses-then-all-act`,
`effect:each-player-chooses-in-turn-order`, `effect:each-player-chooses-creature-type`,
`effect:each-player-chooses-counters-then-goad`, `effect:targets-from-single-graveyard`,
`effect:target-in-that-players-graveyard`, `effect:targets-from-target-players-graveyard`,
`effect:targets-same-controller`, `effect:repeat-process`, `effect:repeat-effect-x-times`.

### Damage prevention and redirection — built 2026-10-28

The need-keys Phase 2 family `replacement:damage-modification` (B250), as
three general pieces in `Game.dealDamage`'s step fold. **Static
`would-deal-damage` replacements** gained a host (`to: "attached"`,
`fromAttached`), "you and your permanents" (`to: "your-side"`, `toOther`),
redirection (`redirect: "self" | "attached" | "recipient-controller"`, rule
614.9, once per event — 614.5), and computed prevention (`preventAmount`,
`preventAllBut`, `preventHalf`, `ifAtMost`) beside `halve`. **Damage that
can't be prevented** (615.12): `damage`'s `cantBePrevented`, the
`damage-cant-be-prevented` effect, the `damageCantBePrevented` static.
**`damage-rule`**: one-shot prevention or redirection over a recipient
scope (a target, players, a permanent filter, an exception) and a source
scope (a target object or spell, a filter, an exception), combat or not, all
/ next time / next N, this turn / until your next turn / while the source
stays, with prevention's follow-up `then` (615.5); chosen-source shields take
`all`, `your-side`/`any` and `then`. Keys:
`replacement:damage-redirection`, `replacement:redirect-next-damage-event`,
the prevent-all-damage and prevent-combat-damage filter keys, the
prevention-follow-up keys, the computed-amount keys, the attached keys,
`effect:prevent-combat-damage-by-target` and the other by-source keys, and
`effect:damage-cant-be-prevented` / `static:damage-cant-be-prevented`.

### Variable target counts — built 2026-10-28

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:variable-target-count` (B245): an
`any-number` group's `max`/`min` as any amount on a triggered or activated
ability, read as its targets are chosen (`specsWithAmounts`); a total mana
value cap (`maxTotalManaValue`) and `differentManaValues`; a group in a
`castModal` mode, with the client asking X after a modal spell's modes. Keys:
`decision:variable-target-count`, `decision:x-targets`,
`decision:target-total-mana-value`, `decision:any-number-targets-in-mode`.
Still open beside them: `decision:twice-x-target-count` (a spell's group at
twice X) and `decision:mixed-zone-targets` (one slot over battlefield and
graveyard).

### Losing all abilities, block limits and type-setting effects — built 2026-10-28

Three need-keys Phase 2 families in one build (B249). **A static loss of all
abilities** (`StaticAbility.loseAllAbilities`, layer 6): `hasLostAbilities` /
`abilitiesLostAt` take the state and see static losses beside effects', from
`staticAbilityLosses` in `characteristics.ts` — dependency order (613.8a: a
static whose permanent loses its abilities is gone first; a loop by
timestamp, 613.8b), what the same static grants after the loss, an applied
loss keeping its other parts in the later layers (613.6, Humility made a
creature — `staticOutlivesLoss`), memoized per mutation (`anyLoseAllSource`,
on `invalidateComputedCache`'s epoch). Beside it, static `addColors`,
`setsChosenColor` and `loseLandTypes`. **Block limits** (`blockAdditional`
on a static and the `block-additional` effect, rule 509.1a): the blockers
offer's `maxBlocks`, a blocker named once per attacker, `blockingAlso`, its
damage divided (510.1d), "whenever ~ blocks" once (509.3a); the client's
block builder takes a blocker up again for each more. **"Becomes a [type]"**:
`add-types`' `setTypes` / `setSubtypes` (205.1a), and `choose-creature-type`'s
`exclude`. Keys: `static:static-lose-all-abilities`,
`static:static-loses-all-abilities`, `static:static-set-characteristics`,
`static:set-characteristics-aura`, `effect:block-any-number-this-turn`,
`static:block-additional-creature`, `static:can-block-any-number`,
`effect:set-creature-type`, `effect:set-types-temporary`.

### Searching another player's zones — built 2026-10-28

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:search-opponent-library` (B248):
`search-library`'s `whose` (a player target, a target's controller or owner,
the trigger player) — the searcher decides, the library's owner shuffles,
battlefield finds enter under the searcher's control; `zones:
"graveyard-hand-library"` for the extract family, the hand shown to the
searcher; `all` (graveyard matches taken, hidden zones' optional — rule
701.23b); exile destinations, face down with `playable` (Praetor's Grasp),
and `"stay"` for a cast from the library (Knowledge Exploitation); `then`
over the finds with the searched player as "that player". Also: the
`exiled-from-hand` this-way kind, the `sameNameAsThisWay` filter (Haunting
Echoes), `EffectPlayerRef`'s `ownerOfTarget`, a countered spell's controller
kept as it leaves the stack (`lastStackController`, Counterbore's ruling),
and the `target` condition's `now` (Deicide's "the exiled card"). Keys:
`effect:search-opponent-library`, `effect:extract-same-name`.

### Banding — built 2026-10-28

The need-keys Phase 2 family `keyword:banding` (B247): rule 702.22 whole —
bands declared with the attack (`AttackerDeclaration.band`, validated by
`combat/banding.ts`'s `whyBandsInvalid`: banding and up to one without, or a
shared quality one member "bands with other" of, one defender), a blocker of
one member blocking the band (`GameObject.blockingAlso`, `blocker-declared`'s
`alsoBlocked` for 509.3a-b), a blocker dividing its damage among the band
(510.1d — `assign-combat-damage`'s `blocking`), and the other side dividing
damage when banding is involved (702.22j-k — `forOpponent`, answered by
default with `opposedAssignment`). Also: the `banding` and `bands-with-other`
keywords, the `bandsWithOther` static, the `bandedWithSource` filter. Key:
`keyword:banding`. Still open beside it: 702.22i ("would become blocked due
to an effect" — no effect makes an attacker blocked yet).

### Who may look at what — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys family `zone:visibility-extensions`. `StaticAbility.visibility`,
read by `viewFor` alone: hands played revealed (Telepathy, Wandering Eye),
library tops played revealed by "players" (Wizened Snitches), looking at
face-down creatures you don't control (Keeper of the Lens, Found Footage,
until end of turn by a `grant-static` — Lumbering Laundry), and the own-library
look as a grantable static (Glowcap Lantern). `look-at-library-top` is the
one-shot look at another library's top card (Mishra's Bauble, Merfolk Observer);
Peek shows `look-at-hand` had the rest of it. What it doesn't build: Sen
Triplets' casting from another player's hand, and "this turn" hands revealed
that only that card needs.

### A trigger's recipient as a reference — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `trigger:trigger-recipient-ref` +
`trigger:becomes-blocked-attacked-recipient`. `"trigger-recipient"` joined
`EffectTargetRef`, so every effect that takes a reference reaches what the
triggering event was aimed at, not just `destroy`, `tap`, `exile` and `damage`
(Permeating Mass's `become-copy`, Obelisk Spider's `add-counter`, Neko-Te's
lasting `restrict`). `DelayedTrigger.recipient` carries it into a delayed
trigger the ability makes (603.7c — Ohran Viper, Sosuke, Lowland Basilisk at
the next combat's end), and `attacker-blocked`, `attacked-alone` and
`attacker-unblocked` now set the recipient `attacks` did (Rakdos Roustabout).

### An unless's payments — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:unless-pay-colored-per-amount`: one
`UnlessOption` per payment (rule 118.12a), each offered only to a player who can
do all of it (118.3): `pay` with `andLife` (Mundungu) and `ifPaid` (Cyclone, its
`times` being "{G} for each wind counter"), `payLifeAmount` (Essence Vortex),
`payEnergy` (Rush of Inspiration), `putCounter`, `returnToHand` (Karoo),
`tapPermanent` (Command Bridge), `sacrificeThat` (Enchanter's Bane, Curse
Artifact). They go on an `each-player-may` too. Keys: the group's nine. Still
open beside it: the Chants' trigger (`trigger:player-puts-onto-battlefield`), and
Reservoir Kraken's "any opponent may … If they do", where the option is built
but how several opponents answer isn't.

### Attack taxes past a fixed {N} on a player — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `cost:attack-tax`: `attackTax`'s `covers` (you,
your planeswalkers, or both), `generic` as a live `CountSpec`, `life`, and the
`player-effect` effect's `attackTax` for a while (`covers: "all"` is War Tax's
every attack). The offer's rates are per defender, a planeswalker's included,
with `lifePerCreature` and `lifeBudget` beside the mana. Keys: `cost:attack-tax`,
`cost:attack-tax-amount`, `cost:attack-tax-life`, `cost:attack-tax-planeswalkers`.
Still open beside it: a Phyrexian tax (Norn's Annex).

### A cost on a particular creature to attack or block — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `cost:attack-tax-per-counters` + `static:attack-cost-for-creature`
+ `static:block-tax`, with `cost:attack-block-cost-self` riding along:
`StaticAbility.combatTax` (`combat/tax.ts`) on the creatures a static reaches, on
attacking, blocking or being blocked, fixed, by a `CountSpec` or per counter on
the taxed creature, narrowed to defenders (`covers`) or attackers (`attacker`);
the `player-effect` effect's `blockTax` (War Cadence). The `declare-attackers`
offer's `attackTax.perAttacker` and the `declare-blockers` offer's `blockTax`
carry the rates; a requirement never obliges paying. Proving cards: Brainwash,
Phyrexian Marauder, Nils, Cowed by Wisdom, Qal Sisma Behemoth, Hipparion, Awesome
Presence, War Cadence, Archangel of Tithes. Still open: a cost that isn't mana or
life (Hollow Warrior's tap, a `declare-attackers` choice), and the client's running
cost line doesn't add `perAttacker` or show a block's yet (the engine refuses what
can't be paid).

### A can't-attack restriction scoped to the defender — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `static:cant-attack-you-player-only` +
`effect:creatures-cant-attack-you-until-next-turn` + `static:cant-attack-same-player`:
`cantAttackController: "player"` (the player alone, not their planeswalkers), the
`player-effect` effect's `cantAttack`, and the restriction
`cant-attack-player-attacked-this-turn` (`GameObject.attackedPlayersThisTurn`).
`cantAttackController` no longer stops an attack on a battle its controller protects
(neither form names one). Proving cards: Reverence, Blazing Archon, Teferi's Moat,
Queen Mother Ramonda, Elephant Grass (and its tax by colour), Heat Wave, Chronomantic
Escape, Port Razer, Bloodthirster. Beside it: `effect:player-cant-attack-you-this-turn`
and `-next-turn`, a prohibition on one player, are a group of their own.

### Statics acting on a player — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `static:static-over-enchanted-player` and
`static:static-you-cant-win-opponents-cant-lose`, with `who: "enchanted-player"`
(Curse of Exhaustion's `prohibits`) generalised. The player a Curse enchants now
also reaches `CardFilter.controlledBy` (so every static with a filter scope: Curse of
Death's Hold's P/T, Curse of the Nightly Hunt's `must-attack`, Curse of Conformity's
base P/T and new `loseCreatureTypes`), a `would-gain-life` or `would-mill` replacement's `who`,
a `would-deal-damage` replacement's `to` (Curse of Bloodletting), and
`deals-damage-batch`'s `to` (Grievous Wound, `trigger:enchanted-player-dealt-damage`).
`cantWinGame` and `opponentsCantLoseGame` are Platinum Angel's pair the other way
round (Abyssal Persecutor), read by `playerCantWinGame`/`playerCantLoseGame`. Still
needing their own keys or checks: Curse of Hospitality's "creatures attacking enchanted
player", and the double-faced Radiant Grace, Faithbound Judge and Accursed Witch, for
`author-freed`; the two `amount:curses-attached-to-player` Curses wait on that key.

### Costs that exile a chosen object from a named place — built 2026-11-01

The group `cost:exile-card-from-hand` (+ `cost:exile-creature-cost`,
`cost:exile-from-single-graveyard`, `cost:exile-spell-you-control`), no new decision. A
permanent you control is `sacrifice` + `exilesSacrifice` (`AbilityCost` and `additionalCost`):
the same offer and action field (the offer's `sacrifice.exile` says so to the client), read
back as `"sacrificed"`, but no sacrifice event and what can't be sacrificed still pays.
`exileFromHand { count, filter? }` asks with `choose-from-zone`; a mana ability waits for the
answer and adds its mana then (`thenManaAbility`, rule 605.3b). `exileFromGraveyard.singleGraveyard`
is any player's graveyard holding enough, all from one (a `share` rule, a card's tag its owner).
`exileSpell { filter? }` takes a spell or copy of yours off the stack. Proving: Food Chain, City of
Shadows, Necrotic Fumes, Cadaverous Bloom, Night Soil, Nivmagus Elemental. Left on the keys' cards:
Holistic Wisdom's `condition:shares-card-type-with-cost-exiled`, Curie's copy of the exiled
creature and base power, Soul Exchange's +2/+2 counter (no such counter's P/T), Synthesis Pod's
reveal-until from the exiled spell's mana value, Fear of Abduction's return to hand.

### Alternative costs paid in life or a life change — built 2026-11-01

The group `cost:alt-cost-opponent-gains-life` (+ `cost:alt-cost-pay-life-equal-mana-value`,
`cost:alternative-cost-pay-life`). `alternativeCost.othersGainLife` ("have an opponent / each
other player gain N life"; a player who can't gain life can't pay it, one whose gain is
replaced can: `LifeGainReplacement.gainsNoLifeInstead`). Life equal to the mana value:
`alternativeCostForSpells.payLifeManaValue` (with `oncePerTurn`, `yourTurnOnly`, `fromHand`),
`grant-spells-this-turn`'s, `impulse-exile`'s (the only way to cast). New with them, a real
choice among alternative costs (rules 118.9a, 601.2b): `Game.alternativeCostsOf`, a cast
variant per cost or per opponent, `altCostIndex` naming one past the first; and kicker on top
of any of them (118.9d). Proving: Snuff Out, Invigorate, Reverent Silence, Demon of Fate's
Design, Marshland Bloodcaster, Inside Information. Left on the key's cards: Nashi's "you may
play **one of** those cards" (the permission lapsing on the others once one is played), and
`cast-now` has no "by paying life" yet for Anrakyr (which also needs
`effect:cast-now-from-hand-or-graveyard`).

### Tapping a counted or totalled set — built 2026-11-01

The group `cost:tap-x-untapped-cost` (+ `cost:tap-creatures-sharing-creature-type`,
`effect:tap-x-then`, `cost:tap-cost-transform-trigger`). `tapOthers.count: "x"` is
offered once per X, each with its own `tapCost.count` and what that X's mana leaves;
`shareCreatureType` puts each choice's subtypes on the offer (`creatureTypes`) and
refuses a pick with nothing in common. The effect forms needed nothing new. New with
them, `manaFrom.fromSource` and `ManaOrigin.object`/`stint`. Proving: Secluded
Starforge, Necron Overlord, Aryel, Weight of Conscience, Thousand Moons Smithy //
Barracks of the Thousand. Left on the key's cards: Resonance Technician's "target
spell with mana value X" (a `stack` target doesn't read X yet), Cryptic Gateway's
`filter:shares-creature-type-with-this-way`, Invasion of New Phyrexia's emblem.

### Sacrifice costs of a number, in parts, and in a mana ability — built 2026-11-01

The group `cost:sacrifice-multiple` (+ `cost:sacrifice-x-mana-ability`,
`cost:tap-creatures-total-power`), with `cost:flashback-sacrifice-x` and
`replacement:regenerate-instead-of-destroy` riding along. `SacrificeCost`'s
`{ parts: [{ filter, count }] }` gives each kind its own number or `"x"`,
`differentNames` a total constraint, asked one at a time; a mana ability may now
sacrifice several (`pendingCostSacrifice.manaAbility`: the mana comes as the last
is chosen, off the stack, rule 605.3b); "any number of" and "one or more" are `"x"`
and `minX`; `flashback.sacrifice.count: "x"`. "X mana of any one color" is offered
per colour. `regeneratesInstead` is rule 701.19b. Proving: Springjack Pasture,
Emrakul's Evangel, Glass-Cast Heart, Transmutation Font, Firecat Blitz, Mossbridge
Troll. Left on the key's cards: "up to three" (`maxX`, Baba Lysaga), a spell's
Plumb the Forbidden copies, Lich-Knights' Conquest's sacrifice as an effect.

### A graveyard's order — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys `effect:top-card-of-graveyard`, `effect:top-creature-card-of-graveyard`
and `effect:reorder-graveyard`. A graveyard's list already ended at the top of the
pile (rules 404.1, 404.2). The readers: the `top-of-graveyard` effect (`whose`,
`filter`, `ifIt`, `bottom`, `then` as target 0) and `exileFromGraveyard`'s `fromTop`
cost. Rule 404.3: cards put into one graveyard at once (`withGraveyardEnterBatch`) are
arranged by their owner at arrival, before priority, with the existing
`choose-from-zone` `order`, and only in a game where some card in any zone reads the
order (`readsGraveyardOrder`, from the definition's data). `reorder-graveyard` is the
same question over the whole pile. Proving: Soldevi Digger, Guiding Spirit, Phyrexian
Furnace, Alms, Necratog, Corpse Dance, Fossil Find. Still needing their own code:
Spinning Darkness's alternative cost, Bösium Strip's cast from the top, Phyrexian
Grimoire's choice among the top two; Barrow Ghoul and Circling Vultures's "sacrifice
unless you exile" and Bone Dancer's defending player are for `author-freed` to check.

### The most common color among permanents — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys family `condition:most-common-color-among-chosen-player-permanents`
(with `condition:most-common-color-among-all-permanents`): one `StaticCondition`,
`most-common-color`, in `characteristics.ts`. Each permanent counts once per color
at layer 5 (`effectiveColors`, so a P/T static asks without folding), a token stack
once per token; a color no permanent has isn't the most common. `color` (a color or
the source's chosen one), `sharedBy` (`"attached"`, or `{ target }` at resolution),
`among` (`nontoken`, `chosenPlayer`) and `untied`. Proving: Ruham Djinn, Heroic
Defiance, Tsabo's Assassin, Barrin's Unmaking, Call to Arms.

### Sacrificing or discarding instead of entering — built 2026-11-01

The top group of the need-keys family `replacement:new-event-replacements`
(`replacement:enter-replacement-discard`, `enter-replacement-sacrifice`).
`EnterInstead` on an `enters-battlefield` replacement: asked before it moves
however it enters (rule 614.12), with the existing `choose-permanents` and
`choose-from-zone`. `sacrifice: { filter, count }` is the Ice Age lands' "sacrifice
a Plains instead" and Scorched Ruins's "two untapped lands" (as many as there are,
rule 609.3, but only the whole number gets it in), and `count: "all"` Sheltered
Valley's and Overlaid Terrain's, which enter regardless; `discard` is Mox Diamond.
One that doesn't enter goes to its owner's graveyard unannounced. Proving: Mox
Diamond, Scorched Ruins, Kjeldoran Outpost, Sheltered Valley, Overlaid Terrain.
Still waiting on `{T}, Sacrifice a Swamp` as a mana ability: Lake of the Dead. A
token copy isn't asked (`docs/engine-gaps.md`), so one of these enters free.

### Copying on the way in, past a permanent — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `replacement:copy-on-enter-options`. `CopyOnEnter`
now reads a card in a graveyard or exile (`from`, asked as a `choose-from-zone`
with `copyOnEnterOf`, so a client shows those cards in its popup — Body Double,
Echoing Deeps, Activated Sleeper, The Master, Formed Anew), a `condition` (raid —
Protean Raider), the linked `whenYouDo` (rules 603.11, 707.9g — Superior
Spider-Man, Wall of Stolen Identity), a counter's `ifYouControlCopied` (Undercover
Operative) and `ownPt` (Hulking Metamorph, printed or prototype); Imposter Mech and
Copy Land needed only cards. `BecomesOnEnter` is "it becomes your choice of …" and
Molten Sentry's coin, a copiable value (rule 707.2 — Primal Clay, Corrupted
Shapeshifter). Keys: `replacement:copy-on-enter-options`,
`as-enters-choose-characteristics`, `clone-as-vehicle`, `copy-on-enter-own-pt`,
`enter-as-copy-of-exiled-card`, `enter-as-copy-of-graveyard-card`. Still open:
`clone-exception-if-embalmed` (a token copy isn't asked, `docs/engine-gaps.md`),
`others-enter-as-copy` and the one-shot `next-enter-as-copy`,
`next-enter-with-counters`; Aquamorph Entity's "or is turned face up" and The
Mimeoplasm's two exiled cards aren't covered by what's marked.

### A player's number against yours — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `condition:opponent-more-life` + `condition:you-more-life-than-an-opponent` +
`condition:target-player-more-cards-in-hand-than-you` + `condition:target-player-more-life-than-you`,
and `amount:opponents-with-more-cards-in-hand`: all already said by `player-compare` (since
2026-10-30) and `countPlayers`' `where`, and triaged before them, so no engine code. "That player
or that planeswalker's controller" is `than: { controllerOfTarget: N }`. New: the scaffolder reads
the comparison as a spell's own sentence, after an instruction and as an intervening-if
(`playerComparisonOf`), and a spell's "return ~ to its owner's hand" from the stack. Proving cards:
Linvala, the Preserver; Glorious Enforcer; Pulse of the Forge; Pulse of the Dross; Wojek Investigator.

### What has happened this turn, as an amount — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys group `effect:counters-put-this-turn-stat` + `effect:turn-stat-counters-put` +
`effect:total-mana-value-cast-this-turn` + `effect:turn-stat-cards-discarded` +
`effect:turn-stat-lands-entered`, then `effect:count-players-with-turn-history` +
`effect:turn-stat-per-player-amount`, and `amount:opponents-attacked-this-turn`. New: counters put
(`TurnHistory.countersPut`, the `countersPutThisTurn` amount and `counters-put-this-turn` condition,
over one `CountersPutSpec`: who, which kind, on what), `castThisTurn`'s `total` and
`other`, `turnStat`'s `who: "each"`, and the stat `"opponents-attacked"`. Already there: discards
and lands through `turnHistory` and `countPlayers`' `where`. Proving cards: Iridescent Hornbeetle,
Fairgrounds Trumpeter, Lord Jyscal Guado; Call Forth the Tempest; The Raven Man, Dream Salvage, Hollow
One; Bioengineered Future; Wound Reflection, Smuggler's Share; Militant Angel, Rose, Cutthroat Raider.
`CountersPutSpec` holds a type and "yours", not a whole `CardFilter`, because a placement keeps only
its permanent's types and controller, and no object id: a list naming objects keeps a token from
folding back into its stack (`referencedObjectIds`). So "a counter on this creature" isn't said:
Fractal Tender and Beast, Erudite Aerialist need a mark on the object that token folding respects.

### Choosing a card name — built 2026-10-28

The need-keys Phase 2 family `decision:choose-card-name` (B246): rule 201.4's
choice as a permanent enters (`chooseCardNameOnEnter`, with a look at an
opponent's hand or each opponent's revealed hand first) or as a spell or
ability resolves (the `choose-card-name` effect, `CHOSEN_CARD_NAME` in its
`then`, `remember` to keep it on the source); `GameObject.chosenNames`; the
`CardFilter` clauses `hasName` and `chosenName`; `prohibits` with
`exceptManaAbilities`, `anyZone` and `lands`; a `player-effect`'s `cantCast`
and `reduceSpells.allCasters`; cost statics for an enchanted player and for
abilities of sources in every zone. Key: `decision:choose-card-name`. Still
open beside it: searching another player's zones for the name (the
Memoricide family, `effect:search-opponent-library`), a card type rather than
a name (`decision:choose-card-type`), and player protection from a name
(Runed Halo, `effect:player-protection`).

### Unbounded targeting — built 2026-09-26

**Built** as the `any-number` target spec, a group that is the last slot of
its list (see AUTHORING §7). Re-measured against the top 5000 before it was
built: 36 cards say "any number of target", four times the 9 below, which is
what the revisit line asked for. The desynchronisation described below is
avoided by never re-deriving the group's shape from the board: every check
of a choice expands the group from the number of targets actually chosen
(`concreteTargetSpecs`), and a spell or ability keeps that number with the
targets it went on the stack with. It shipped with Eerie Interlude, Brago,
Divine Resilience, Mindbreak Trap, Court of Cunning, Riverchurn Monument,
Singularity Rupture, Deepglow Skate and Stonespeaker Crystal. Still blocked
among the 36: divided damage or counters as the caster chooses (Fire Covenant, Lathiel,
Vorinclex's saga; Fury now only for its evoke cost — a spell's divided damage landed
2026-10-01 with Magma Opus, an ability's 2026-10-03 with Dragonlord Atarka), phasing (Clever Concealment, Guardian of Faith), a
constraint relating the chosen cards (Ancient Brass Dragon, Rampaging Yao
Guai, V.A.T.S.), and one-offs each needing something of their own. Since
2026-09-28 Fireball's "divided evenly" (`damage-divided-evenly`) and a cost
for each target beyond the first (`costPerExtraTarget` — Fireball, and
Strive's Twinflame and Call the Coppercoats) are built too; an `{X}` spell's
offer then carries the largest X at each target count.

The original scoping, kept for the reasoning:

The headline count is misleading. Of the 11 cards, only four are unblocked by
variable-arity targeting *alone* — Mindbreak Trap (#544), Eerie Interlude
(#971), Priest of Forgotten Gods (#1396), Deepglow Skate (#1829). The rest are
blocked on something else regardless: phasing (Clever Concealment), a d20 roll
(Ancient Brass Dragon), Strive (Twinflame, Call the Coppercoats), divided
damage (Shatterskull Smashing), or a constraint *relating chosen cards to each
other* (Agadeem's Awakening — explicitly unsupported, see the limitations list).

Against that, it is the most invasive change available. Every effect addresses
targets by fixed slot index (`target: 0`), `ResolvedTargets` is a flat array
with one entry per declared spec, and `effectiveTargetSpecs` is re-derived **at
resolution** for the fizzle check — so a slot count that depends on the board
would desynchronise between cast and resolution, which is precisely when the
board has changed. Expanding "any number" into N optional slots at enumeration
time has the same problem from the other end.

Four cards is not worth that. Revisit if the count grows, or alongside Strive
(which needs a per-target cost and would want the same machinery).

## The limitation ledger — every §15 gap, ranked by blocked cards

Measured 2026-09-20, the day AUTHORING §0 ("faithful, or not at all") landed.
One Scryfall oracle-text search per limitation in AUTHORING §15, joined
against the *unimplemented* entries of `top-commander-cards.txt`. Read each
number as an **upper bound on what that one gap gates**: a hit may be blocked
on something else as well, and the searches are textual.

The point of the table is that §15 is not a flat list. Two entries gate 40
cards between them and the bottom half gates one or none — so "is this
limitation reasonable to keep?" has a different answer per row, and until now
nothing in the repo distinguished them.

| limitation | blocked | best rank | ≤100 | ≤500 |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| **mana provenance** (all three shapes) | 21 | **14** | 1 | 7 |
| ~~protection from [filter]~~ *(the filter half built 2026-09-21; granted and chosen-colour 2026-10-28; player protection still open)* | 19 | 92 | 1 | 4 |
| ~~unbounded targeting~~ *(built 2026-09-26 — see above)* | 9 | 544 | 0 | 0 |
| ~~regeneration~~ *(built 2026-09-28 — Mortivore)* | 7 | 705 | 0 | 0 |
| ~~Station~~ *(built — Hearthhull, the Worldseed)* | 7 | 951 | 0 | 0 |
| ~~"put into a graveyard from anywhere" trigger~~ *(built 2026-09-24 — `put-into-graveyard`; The Gitrog Monster)* | 5 | 259 | 0 | 1 |
| "as this enters" on a non-cast permanent | 5 | 132 | 0 | 1 |
| discard-a-card as an ability cost | 5 | 834 | 0 | 0 |
| ~~damage doubling (a replacement)~~ *(built — `would-deal-damage`; Wolverine's `fromSelf` 2026-09-28)* | 4 | 794 | 0 | 0 |
| another player's graveyard → hand | 4 | 620 | 0 | 0 |
| ~~Hideaway~~ *(built 2026-10-02 — Mosswort Bridge, Spinerock Knoll, Windbrisk Heights and six more)* | 3 | 195 | 0 | 1 |
| ~~Offspring~~ *(built 2026-09-28)* | 3 | 793 | 0 | 0 |
| ~~Warp~~ *(built 2026-09-28)* | 3 | 1387 | 0 | 0 |
| phasing | 3 | 575 | 0 | 0 |
| snow sources | 3 | 445 | 0 | 1 |
| ~~multikicker~~ *(built 2026-10-02 — Everflowing Chalice)* | 2 | 254 | 0 | 1 |
| plays-a-land trigger | 1 | 899 | 0 | 0 |
| Bestow / ~~Eternalize~~ *(built 2026-09-28)* / Coven / Raid | 1 each | 697+ | 0 | 0 |
| retrace / ~~riot~~ *(built 2026-10-03, printed riot — Skarrgan Hellkite)* / Prototype | **0** | — | 0 | 0 |

Retrace, riot and Prototype gate **nothing** in the top 2000 — they are in §15
because a card in the pool wanted them, not because the backlog does. Leave
them.
Since measured: mana provenance and the plays-a-land trigger are built.

## Built ahead: vocabulary no card uses yet

Measured on 2026-10-09 by grepping the 8,490 card files in `cards/pool` and
the 249 in `cards/tokens` (the first count, 2026-09-25, listed about twice as
many pieces). Each piece below was built ahead of a card that is still in the
backlog (most of them in the 2026-09-24 gap wave), so nothing in the pool runs
it and the fuzzer never reaches it. Keep it, and give the first card that uses
one a close rules review. The pieces marked **untested** have no unit test
either, so nothing has ever run them.

- **Trigger kinds:** the `predicate` escape hatch (**untested**).
- **Static conditions:** `source-greatest`.
- **Filter clauses:** three of the card-property clauses built beside
  Raggadragga's and Duskana's: `hasAbilities` (Jasmine Boreal of the Seven),
  `coloredManaSymbols` (Omnath, Locus of All) and `nameDiffersFromEach`
  (Light-Paws, Emperor's Voice) — each unit-tested in
  `card-property-clauses.test.ts`, none yet in a card.
- **Amounts:** `{ damageDealtThisTurn }`.
- **Target spec:** `creature-or-player` (**untested**). One real card still
  prints "target creature or player": Firesong and Sunspeaker.
- **Replacement fields:** `transformed` on enters-battlefield (the cards that
  return transformed use `flicker`'s or `put-onto-battlefield`'s
  `transformed` instead). Also `painIfUntapped` (**untested**), the one piece
  no real card can use: it was written for a Rockfall Vale text that isn't the
  card's, and no card in the Oracle snapshot deals damage for entering
  untapped.
- **Goad and suspect** (2026-09-25, `goad-extensions.test.ts`,
  `suspect.test.ts`): the `unsuspect` effect, and `attack-requirement`'s
  `otherThanYou: false`.
- **Optional fields no card sets yet:**
  - Effects: `return-from-graveyard.withCounters`,
    `additional-combat.withMain`, `become-monarch.who`, `get-energy.who`,
    `prevent-damage.combatOnly`.
  - Triggers: `transforms.filter`, `chapter-resolves.filter`,
    `deals-combat-damage-to-player.otherOnly`, `blocks.otherOnly`,
    `becomes-blocked.otherOnly`, `put-into-exile.filter`,
    `dealt-damage.combat`.
  - Conditions: `cards-in-exile.filter`, `damage-dealt-this-turn.who`.

Since used by a card, so off the list: the `day-night` effect (Tovolar, Dire
Overlord), `gain-control-all` (Dihada, Binder of Wills and three more), the
`player-counters` condition, `canBlockOnly`, `notColors`, `notName`,
`sharesCardTypeWith`, `xInManaCost`, `cardTypeCount`, `EVERY_LAND_TYPE` (Omo,
Queen of Vesuva), `{ sum }`, `from` on would-be-put-into-graveyard, `plus` on
would-deal-damage, `PtModifier.addColors` (set by an `enterAs` or copy
exception's `addColors`), `goad.forGame`, `create-token`'s `goadedForGame`,
`suspect` of `"source"`, and the optional fields `add-mana.persists`,
`exile-from-library.amount`, `reveal-until.tapped`, `animate-all.keywords`,
`create-token-copy.gainUntilEndOfTurn`, `add-player-counters.target`,
`modal.notChosenThisTurn`, `may.costEnergy`, `may.else`,
`put-onto-battlefield.under`, `attacks.defender` and
`.aloneAgainstDefender`, `blocks.filter`, `becomes-blocked.filter`,
`deals-damage.toFilter` and `.toItsTarget`, `becomes-tapped.filter`,
`cast-spell.copyOnly` and `.notFrom`, `hand-size.atLeast`, `life-total.who`
and `.atMost`, `turn-structure.steps`, `turn-history.who` and `.atLeast`, and
`this-way.atLeast`. (Rechecked 2026-09-28: `prohibit`, `cant-be-sacrificed`,
`attacks-player` and `castAsThoughFlash` had cards by then.)

Engine-internal pieces aren't listed: the `return-flickered` effect, and the
`progress` field on `reveal-until` and `each-player-may`, are made by the
engine itself.

## Done: index of finished passes

Kept so that source comments citing them resolve. The narrative for each is
in `git log`.

- **Tier 1 (EDH-popularity backlog)** — Channel (Boseiju, Otawara, Takenuma,
  Eiganjo; `channel.test.ts`), Overload (Cyclonic Rift, Vandalblast, Damn;
  `overload.test.ts`), conditional free-cast (Fierce Guardianship, Deadly
  Rollick, Flawless Maneuver; `free-cast.test.ts`), "Double" (Kalonian Hydra,
  Bristly Bill, Unnatural Growth — `double-counters-all`/`double-pt-all`,
  which also filled the gap P16's dropped World War Hulk chapter III had
  flagged; `double.test.ts`), Convoke (Hour of Reckoning, Chord of Calling;
  `convoke.test.ts`).
- **Tier 1b (primitives the keyword screen missed)** — delayed triggered
  abilities (`delayed-triggers.test.ts`), put a card on top of a library
  (`library-top.test.ts`), put a card from hand onto the battlefield
  (`put-from-hand.test.ts`), additional costs beyond a sacrifice incl. a
  choice between two costs (`additional-costs.test.ts`), the top-80 cluster
  (Exotic Orchard, Fellwar Stone, Brainstorm, Swan Song, Reliquary Tower,
  Reanimate, Rhystic Study).
- **E1** — 53 top-200 staples with no new feature (`edh-staples.test.ts`);
  found the `becomes-tapped` trigger and "dies matches every battlefield →
  graveyard move".
- **E2** — every invented placeholder card replaced with a real printing.
- **E3** — five more cycles and the text-structure audit (`card:verify --text`).
- **Token copies' "as this enters" choices** (`bug:token-copy-skips-as-enters-choices`, `bug:token-copy-aura-attach-choice`, 2026-11-02) —
  a token copy settles them off the battlefield, each token for itself, then all of one
  instruction's enter together through `moveObject` (`token-copy-as-enters.test.ts`): Rebuild the
  City, Kindred Charge, Ember Island Production.
- **Skipping a turn as it would begin** (`replacement:skip-extra-turns`, `replacement:skip-turn`, 2026-11-02) —
  the `would-begin-turn` replacement, by whose turn and whether extra, with a "you may" asked as it would
  begin (`skip-turn-replacement.test.ts`): Time Vault, Gerrard's Hourglass Pendant; Chronatog and Magosi, the
  Waterveil needed no code.
- **Curses** (`mechanic:curses`, 2026-10-05, TDC6) — an Aura that enchants a
  player (`GameObject.attachedToPlayer`, rule 303.4, with the 303.4f choice of
  player), "whenever enchanted player is attacked" (`curseWhenAttacked`) and
  "each opponent attacking that player does the same", and "a creature
  enchanted player controls" (Curse of Opulence, Verbosity, Bounty,
  Disturbance, Trespasser's Curse; `curses.test.ts`). The rest wait on one
  more feature each (`engine/data/sweep-3/TDC6.json`).
- **Demonstrate** (2026-10-05, TDC7) — rule 702.144a as `demonstrate()`
  (`copy-spell`'s `controller`), with `cast-now`'s `by` and the
  `{ controllerOfTarget }` player reference: Transforming Flourish and the
  Creative, Incarnation and Replication Techniques (`demonstrate.test.ts`).
- **The TDC precons' last four** (2026-10-07, TDC8) — dredge (`keyword:dredge`,
  `CardDefinition.dredge`, asked before each draw), `keep-total-power`, a
  modal's `optional` and `eachTargetsDifferentPlayer`, `add-counter-all`'s
  `putByTarget`, `keepsUnspentMana` and a `may`'s `xColor`: Life from the
  Loam, Slaughter the Strong, Shadrix Silverquill, Leyline Tyrant
  (`precon-tdc-batch-6.test.ts`). Reality Shift still waits on face-down
  permanents.
- **Mana provenance** — the mana pool as tagged `ManaUnit`s (Path of
  Ancestry, Cavern of Souls, …; `mana-provenance.test.ts`, AUTHORING §15).
- **Changeling** (`keyword:changeling`) — every creature type, and every land
  type, as one marker in a subtype list (`subtypes.ts`; Morophon, the
  Boundless, with the `ofChosenType` filter clause for its anthem;
  `changeling.test.ts`). Realmwalker and Mirror Entity, the other Tier 2
  names, each still need pieces of their own.
- **Batched entries** (`trigger:enters-batch`) — `enters-battlefield`'s
  `batched`: "whenever one or more … enter" fires once per simultaneous entry
  (`withEnterBatch`), a compacted token stack counting as its tokens, with
  "that much" as how many matched (Marneus Calgar, Ingenious Artillerist;
  `enters-batch.test.ts`). `create-token-copy`'s `of: "entered-together"`
  is "for each of them, create a copy of it" over that entry, carried in
  `LastKnownRefs.enteredTogether` (`effect:token-copy-options` — Kambal,
  Profiteering Mayor; `token-copy-entered-together.test.ts`).
- **Connive** (`keyword:connive`, rule 701.50) — the `connive` effect: the
  permanent's controller draws N, discards N, and puts a +1/+1 counter on it
  per nonland card of those discards (Raffine, Scheming Seer; Ledger
  Shredder, Spymaster's Vault; `connive.test.ts`).
- **Delayed triggers over what was just made** (part of
  `effect:delayed-trigger-extensions`) — `delayed-trigger`'s `about`, with
  the `"created"` this-way kind: the delayed ability's targets are the
  tokens its resolution created (Satya, Aetherflux Genius;
  `commanders-satya.test.ts`); and "whenever that creature deals combat
  damage to a player this turn", firing each time (Captain Howler, Sea Scourge;
  `commanders-doom-howler.test.ts`, now `at: { whenever, watching }`), which
  finishes the feature.
- **Evoke** (`keyword:evoke`, rule 702.74) — `evoke: { cost }`, the cast's
  `evoke` variant, and the sacrifice trigger given as the permanent enters,
  stacked first so its own enters abilities resolve before it
  (`TriggeredAbility.stackFirst`); `grantsEvokeToSpells` for Ashling, the
  Limitless (Mulldrifter, Shriekmaw, Nulldrifter, Foundation Breaker,
  Reveillark; `evoke.test.ts`, `commanders-ashling.test.ts`). The
  Incarnations' "exile a card from your hand" evoke cost is still open.
- **Casting cards you don't own** (`zone:cast-cards-you-dont-own`) — the
  caster controls the spell (rule 601.2a), a permanent spell resolves under
  them (608.3a), and a land played is the player's; `impulse-exile`'s
  `whose` takes a target slot, and `whileSource` / `oncePerTurn` link the
  permission to its source (Maralen, Fae Ascendant; Agent of Treachery;
  `cast-cards-you-dont-own.test.ts`, `commanders-maralen.test.ts`). An
  impulse permission now ends when its card leaves exile (400.7).
- **Exiled face down** (`zone:exile-face-down`, rule 406.3) —
  `impulse-exile`'s `faceDown`: `GameObject.exiledFaceDown` names who may
  look, `viewFor` hides the card from everyone else, and the public stint the
  move opened is dropped rather than closed, so the history never names it —
  foretell now drops its stint the same way (Edward Kenway;
  `commanders-edward-kenway.test.ts`, `public-stints.test.ts`). Split out of
  `zone:visibility-extensions`, which keeps the revealed hand, the private
  look at a library's top, and reveals to everyone.
- **Top-5000 batch 5** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-5.test.ts`) — the
  Enduring cycle's return "as an enchantment" (`put-onto-battlefield`'s
  `setTypes`, in place as it enters; part of `static:self-type-changes`),
  attaching an Equipment the effect names (`attach`'s `attachment` — Hammer
  of Nazahn, Sigarda's Aid; part of `effect:attach-extensions`), an amount
  picked by a condition (`ifCondition` — the Urza's lands, a mana ability
  that can't be a `conditional`), "a target was chosen" (`target-chosen` —
  The Earth Crystal's two counters over one or two targets), mana that pays
  only for abilities of creature *permanents* unless it says "source"
  (`abilityOfAnyZone` — Castle Garenbrig; Secluded Courtyard), and a check
  land not counting lands that enter beside it (`ConditionOptions.
  notYetHere`). Helpers: `evolve`, `enduringReturn`, `untappedEntryLand`
  (Mystic Sanctuary's cycle, whose "enters untapped" is the trigger's filter
  rather than an intervening-if), `urzaLand`. B5.json lists the 79 cards it
  left blocked.
- **Top-5000 batch 6** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-6.test.ts`) — "spells
  you control can't be countered" (`grantsToSpells.cantBeCountered` —
  Destiny Spinner, Prowling Serpopard, Allosaurus Shepherd, Hexing
  Squelcher), triggered abilities that work in the graveyard
  (`TriggeredAbility.fromGraveyard`, rule 113.6k — Bloodghast; Spit Flame's
  return had never fired), a "you may" that keeps knowing its source became
  a new object (the `choose-modes` decision's `sourceLost`), `animate-all`
  P/T from a live amount (Mirror Entity's X), a subtype added as a card
  enters (`put-onto-battlefield`'s `addSubtypes` — Portal to Phyrexia),
  `plays-land`'s `otherOnly` (City of Traitors), "you have hexproof"
  (`playerHexproof` — Shalai), living weapon (`livingWeapon()`: `attach`'s
  `target: "created"`), and `grantPtPerCount.countersOnSource` (Door of
  Destinies). "Its controller creates …" now comes before the counter (An
  Offer You Can't Refuse, Strix Serenade): a countered copy has ceased to
  exist by then. B6.json lists the 64 cards it left blocked.
- **Top-5000 batch 7** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-7.test.ts`) — unearth
  (`unearth()` — Molten Gatekeeper) and undying as a grantable ability
  (`undying()` — Mikaeus, the Unhallowed), "+1/+1 for each creature card in
  your graveyard" (`grantPtPerCount.inGraveyard` — Wight of the Reliquary),
  Past in Flames' mass flashback (`grant-flashback-all` — and Will of the
  Jeskai, blocked on it in B4), a card with its own flashback and a granted
  one casting with either (`Game.flashbacksOf`, named by `graveyardGrant`, as
  escape already was — Snapcaster Mage had only offered the printed one),
  `counter` and `destroy` of `"trigger-object"` (Vexing Bauble; Mikaeus's
  "destroy it"), `exile-graveyard` of `"each-opponent"` (Soul-Guide
  Lantern), `damage-all`'s `whose` (Balefire Dragon), a `controls`
  condition's `excludeTriggerObject` (Valakut's "five other Mountains"),
  `opponent-controls-more`'s `activePlayerOnly` (Keeper of the Accord),
  `deals-damage`'s `to: "you"`, a `CardFilter`'s `attachedTo` (Conqueror's
  Flail), a permanent target `whose: "defending-player"` (Kogla), a
  one-sided fight at a planeswalker (Stump Stomp), a targeted `lose-life`
  reading the target's own life (Peer into the Abyss), and cycling as the
  discard it is (Archfiend of Ifnir, Waste Not — it had never fired a
  discard trigger). Faithless Looting's flashback was {1}{R}; it's {2}{R}.
  B7.json lists the 50 cards it left blocked.
- **Top-5000 batch 8** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-8.test.ts`) — a filter's
  `notThisWay` and `create-token`'s `separate` (Martial Coup's "destroy all
  **other** creatures" after making its Soldiers, which would otherwise join
  and shield an older Soldier stack), a `this-way` condition's `other`
  (Arid Archway's "if **another** Desert was returned this way"), a
  `look-and-choose` second pick only `ifNoneChosen` (Planar Genesis's "if you
  don't") and `reveal: "chosen"` (Monumental Henge's "you may reveal a
  historic card from among them" — Adaptive Omnitool had been revealing all
  six), `abilityCostModification`'s `leavesOneMana` (Forensic Gadgeteer, and
  Training Grounds, blocked on it in B6) — which now also reaches mana
  abilities, by hand and as the auto-payer prices a converter, and only a
  permanent's abilities (Dauntless Cathar's graveyard ability was reduced) —
  `cast`/`castFrom` asked of a spell still on the stack (Delayed Blast
  Fireball's "if this spell was cast from exile"), and `outlast()` (Abzan
  Falconer). B8.json lists the 54 cards it left blocked.
- **Top-5000 batch 9** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-9.test.ts`) —
  `extraLandsForEachPlayer` (Rites of Flourishing's land drop for every
  player), `would-mill`'s `plus` (The Water Crystal; after any multiplier),
  `would-gain-life`'s `multiplier` (Rhox Faithmender, Boon Reflection, The
  Wind Crystal; after any `plus`), a `castVia` filter clause read off the
  permanent's entry (Uro's "unless it escaped"), and a spell's own "when you
  cast this spell" reading its X (Hydroid Krasis). B9.json lists the 57
  cards it left blocked.
- **Top-5000 batch 10** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-10.test.ts`) — a
  `cards-in-graveyard` condition (Oversold Cemetery's four creature cards),

- **Top-5000 batch 11** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-11.test.ts`) — nothing new: 31

- **Discard as an activation cost** (2026-09-29, `ability-discard-cost.test.ts`) —

- **Top-5000 batch 12** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-12.test.ts`) — 34 cards on the existing

- **Top-5000 batch 13** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-13.test.ts`) — 25 cards on the existing

- **Top-5000 batch 14** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-14.test.ts`) — 26 cards on the existing

- **Top-5000 batch 15** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-15.test.ts`) — 25 cards on the existing

- **Top-5000 batch 17** (2026-09-29, `top5000-batch-17.test.ts`) — 9 cards; a short pass (4 of them from a recheck of its blockers).

- **Top-5000 batch 18** (2026-09-30, `top5000-batch-18.test.ts`) — 41 cards, one engine change:

- **Tarkir: Dragonstorm precons batch 1** (2026-09-30, `precon-tdc-batch-1.test.ts`) — 41 of the

- **Tarkir: Dragonstorm precons batch 2** (2026-09-30, `precon-tdc-batch-2.test.ts`,
  `cast-now.test.ts`) — casting a spell as another resolves (rule 608.2g), most of
  `effect:cast-during-resolution`: the `cast-now` effect grew `from` (a hand, a graveyard, the
  looked-at top N of a library), `spell` (a filter on the spell as it would be cast — 601.3e),
  `free` (an alternative cost of {0} that kicker may be paid on top of — 118.9d), `then` / `else`
  and `rest`, and the decision offers several cards at once. An instant or sorcery now leaves the
  stack only once its parked steps are done (608.2n), `counter` can exile, and a cast trigger's
  "that spell's mana value" reads a spell countered in response as it was on the stack
  (`LastKnownRefs.triggerSpell`). 8 cards; TDC2.json re-triages Transforming Flourish.
- **Tarkir: Dragonstorm precons batch 5** (2026-10-02, `precon-tdc-batch-5.test.ts`,

- **Card-property filter clauses** (`condition:filter-card-property-clauses`)
  — base P/T, mana abilities, any ability, `{X}` and coloured symbols in the
  mana cost, card-type count, a name different from a group (Raggadragga,
  Duskana; `card-property-clauses.test.ts`).
- **Ready now** (2026-10-03, `ready-now-features.test.ts`) — the cards the 10-03 passes
  unblocked, rechecked: copying an activated or triggered ability (`effect:copy-ability`: the

  `count` with `countCastBefore` and `{ commanderCasts: "you" }`, a mana rider's
  `"trigger-spell"`, the `spell-or-permanent` target, `look-and-choose`'s `player:
  "that-player"`, `each-player-may`'s `who: { controllerOfTarget }`, and trigger doublers held
  to permanents' abilities. 18 cards (Thousand-Year Storm, Lithoform Engine, Illusionist's
  Bracers, …); what blocks the other 10 is in
  `docs/card-blockers.md`, "Ready now".
- **Winning and losing** (2026-10-03, `new:win-game` and `effect:lose-game`;
  `win-game.test.ts`, `win-game-features.test.ts`) — `win-game`, `lose-game`,
  "can't lose / can't win" (a static and a turn's `player-effect`), Laboratory
  Maniac's draw replacement, a damage life floor and "can't lose life" (rule
  119.8, every life cost refused), the hand-size fold in timestamp order (rule
  613.11), "Nth from the top", a game-long record of spells cast by name, and
  `gain-control-all`'s `controlledBy: "trigger-player"`. 22 cards: Thassa's
  Oracle, Laboratory Maniac, Jace, Wielder of Mysteries, Platinum Angel, Herald
  of Eternal Dawn, Angel's Grace, Everybody Lives!, Felidar Sovereign, Test of
  Endurance, Revel in Riches, Triskaidekaphile, Knuckles the Echidna, Simic
  Ascendancy, Helix Pinnacle, Hellkite Tyrant, Twenty-Toed Toad, Approach of the
  Second Sun, Pact of Negation, Summoner's Pact, Vorpal Sword, Summon: Primal
  Odin, Mirrodin Besieged. `BACKLOG.md` lists the ones still blocked.
- **"You may sacrifice two …"** (2026-10-06, `effect:may-sacrifice-then`
  extended; `commanders-may-sacrifice-n.test.ts`) — an `UnlessOption`'s
  `sacrifice` takes a `count`, offered only to a player with that many to
  sacrifice (rule 118.3), for `unless` and `each-player-may` alike. Strefan,
  Maurer Progenitor (top-500 commander; his Blood token and every other piece
  already existed) and Rakdos, Patron of Chaos.
- **Attach to the trigger's object or the source, and attach every match**
  (part of `effect:attach-extensions`, 2026-10-10, B46) — `attach`'s `target`
  takes `"trigger-object"` / `"source"` as well as a slot or `"created"`, and
  `attachments: CardFilter` attaches each match (Hero's Blade, Shielded by
  Faith, Sword of the Squeak, Cloud, Ex-SOLDIER, Balan, Wandering Knight;
  `attach-extensions.test.ts`); the `equipmentAttached` filter count; and an
  enters or attacks trigger's object is lost once it has left, back or not
  (rule 400.7). The rest of the feature — attaching to a card just returned,
  to a creature chosen without targeting, "the creature this is attached to"
  as an effect's object, Auras put onto the battlefield attached — is open.
- **Morph, megamorph, disguise and manifest dread** (`mechanic:morph`,
  `mechanic:disguise`, `keyword:manifest-dread`, part of `mechanic:face-down`,
  2026-10-10, B47) — casting face down for {3} (`via: "face-down"`, hidden on
  the stack, 708.4), turning face up for the morph cost (megamorph's counter,
  `asTurnedFaceUp`), `turned-face-up` triggers, the `faceDown` filter, and
  `manifest-dread` (Den Protector, Kadena, Hooded Hydra, Arno Dorian, Trail of
  Mystery, Unwanted Remake; `morph.test.ts`). Open: turning face up by an
  effect, returning face down, manifesting from a hand.
- **Random choice** (`effect:random-choice`, 2026-10-11, B48) — a card at
  random from a graveyard (`choose-random-card`, bound as target 0), an
  opponent or player at random (`choose-opponent`'s `random`/`anyPlayer`), and
  a resolution-time mode at random among those whose `onlyIf` holds (`modal`'s
  `random`), on the seeded stream, logged as `chosen-at-random` (Gamble, Arcane
  Bombardment, Deadbridge Chant, Tomb Tyrant, Wondrous Crucible, Crystalline
  Giant; `random-choice.test.ts`). It had 21 cards waiting, 16 of them on it
  alone by their records; 7 were authorable — the rest each turned out to want
  something more, now keyed: a random card from a hand or among linked exiles,
  a mode at random as an ability goes on the stack. The random opponent is
  built ahead: no pool card picks one yet (Vial Smasher, Indoraptor, Wildfire
  Devils, Explosion of Riches each wait on something else).
- **The Kindred card type** (`mechanic:kindred-card-type`, 2026-10-11, B49) —
  `"kindred"` in `CardType` (rule 308), creature types fitting it (rule 205.3m,
  `subtypeFitsTypes`), changeling reaching it (`withChangeling`), and kindred
  counting as a card type for delirium (`kindred.test.ts`); with it,
  `damage-all`'s `controlledByTarget` (Ashling's Command). 13 of its 19 cards;
  the other 6 each needed another feature, now keyed.
- **Becoming a copy** (`effect:become-copy`, 2026-10-13, B69) — an existing
  permanent, or each one matching a filter, becomes a copy of a permanent, a
  spell or a card (rules 707.2, 707.9, 613.2) for good or until end of turn,
  the next end step or your next turn; `CopyExceptions.thisAbility` for
  "except it has this ability", temporary copies kept in
  `GameObject.temporaryCopies` (`become-copy.test.ts`). 17 of its 21 cards and
  Kimahri; the rest each wait on another feature, now keyed.
- **Moving counters** (`effect:move-counter`, 2026-10-13, B69) — `move-counters`
  (rule 122.5; moving counts as putting, per the rulings) and `put-counters-of`
  for a departed permanent's counters (122.8), graft spelled out
  (`move-counters.test.ts`). 16 of its 21 cards.
- **Devotion and permanents changing their own types** (`condition:devotion`,
  `static:self-type-changes` on the battlefield, 2026-10-13, B70) — `devotionOf`
  (rule 700.5) as a condition, a count and an amount; layer-4 `removeTypes` /
  `setTypes` / chosen creature type (`devotion.test.ts`). 20 Theros Gods and 8
  more; Grist waits on the off-the-battlefield half.
- **Phasing** (`mechanic:phasing`, 2026-10-13, B70) — rule 702.26, phased-out
  permanents kept in `GameState.phasedOut`, with Teferi's Protection's
  player-protection and life-can't-change (`phasing.test.ts`). 16 of its 18 cards.
- **"Whenever a player activates an ability"** (`trigger:activates-ability`,
  2026-10-14) — `activates-ability` with `who: "you" | "opponent"` and
  `ninjutsu`/`loyalty`/`source` narrowing, `grantsNinjutsuInHand`, and a
  `nextSpell` delayed trigger's `orNextAbility` for an {X} activation
  (`commanders-activates-ability.test.ts`). Satoru Umezawa, Magus Lucea Kane,
  Harsh Mentor, Runic Armasaur, Ajani Unrelenting, Way of the Paradox, Gideon
  the Oathless, Rings of Brighthearth. Elrond, Moon-Reader waits on announced
  mana abilities; Way of the Mind Sculptor on "if you removed two or more
  loyalty counters"; Neerdiv on "an ability of a card in your graveyard".
- **Decayed** (`keyword:decayed`, 2026-10-14) — rule 702.147a: the keyword's
  `cant-block` restriction and its own attack trigger (`DECAYED_TRIGGER`),
  decayed counters (122.1b) (`decayed.test.ts`). Wilhelt, the Rotcleaver,
  Crowded Crypt, Ghoulish Procession, Curse of the Restless Dead, Jadar,
  Ghoulcaller of Nephalia. Gisa waits on a linked exile replacement
  (`replacement:exile-instead-linked`), Rot-Curse Rakshasa on X targets.
- **Scry triggers** (`trigger:scry-trigger`, `stat:scried-or-surveilled-this-turn`,
  2026-10-14, B71) — the `scries` trigger (`orSurveil`), a scry or surveil of
  one or more into an empty library counted (rules 701.22d, 701.25d), and the
  `scried-or-surveilled` turn stat (`scry-triggers.test.ts`). Eight cards;
  Elvish Mariner and Elrond, Master of Healing wait on X targets.
- **Reveal a hand, you choose, they discard** (`effect:discard-chosen-from-revealed-hand`,
  2026-10-14, B72) — the `discard-chosen` effect and the `"discard"` zone-choice
  destination (`discard-chosen.test.ts`). Duress, Thoughtseize, Inquisition of
  Kozilek, Coercion and six more; Mathemagics with `{ powerOfTwo }` beside it.
- **"Costs {N} less if it targets …"** (`cost:cost-reduction-if-targets`, 2026-10-14) —
  a card's `targetCostReduction`, priced by the chosen targets (rules 601.2c,
  601.2f), its offer narrowed to the targets that earn it when only the
  reduced price is affordable (`target-cost-reduction.test.ts`). Price of Fame,
  Magnificent End, Thing Swing, Fantastic Bounce; Brush Off waits on a coloured
  reduction, Uneasy Partings on its owner's top-or-bottom choice.
- **Protection granted for a while, and the colour of your choice**
  (`effect:grant-protection-choice`, `effect:grant-protection-temporary`,
  2026-10-28, B244 — need-keys Phase 2's first family) — the `grant-protection`
  effect (rule 702.16): a target and/or every permanent a filter reaches as it
  resolves, one timestamp, layer 6 (`PtModifier.protection`); a fixed quality
  or one chosen as it resolves (`{ choose: "color" | "card-type", or?,
  optional? }`, a `choose-modes` decision); a filter's side fixed to the
  controller's opponents as it resolves (`CardFilter.controllerIn`). The
  static `protection.chosenColor` / `doesntRemoveSource` (rule 702.16n) for
  the chosen-colour Auras (`grant-protection.test.ts`). Gods Willing, Giver of
  Runes, Brave the Elements, Flickering Ward, Pippin and 57 more: 62 of the family's 72.

- **A cost paid a chosen number of times** (`decision:pay-cost-up-to-n-times`,
  2026-11-02) — `may.costTimes`, with `maxX` for "up to three times": no new
  decision, the `choose-modes` offer's `xCost` from 1 naming the cost in `times`,
  paid as one payment (`repeatedManaCost`), the number the X a `reflexive-trigger`
  reads as its counters, `any-number` `max: "x"` or announced `modal` `maxModes:
  "x"` (rule 603.12a; `pay-cost-times.test.ts`). Intrepid Adversary, Primal
  Adversary, Tranquil Frillback.

- **Remove all of a kind of counter as a cost** (`cost:remove-all-counters`,
  2026-11-02) — no engine code: `removeCounter`'s `count: "all"` (Catti-brie of
  Mithral Hall) already removes them as the cost is paid, the number the X its
  effect reads (`remove-all-counters-cost.test.ts`). Molten Hydra, Essence Bottle,
  Geometric Nexus. The scaffolder reads "2 life for each" and "-1/-1 for each" too.

## Completed: `neededCards.txt` passes (P0-P20)

Two curated precon decks (a fixed-dual-land manabase deck, a
lands-in-graveyard deck), ~170 cards, effectively done. `git log` has the full
reasoning per pass; this table is a compact index only.

| pass | shipped | headline addition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| first pass | 8 cards | baseline pool (Birds of Paradise, Nature's Lore, ...) |
| P0 | 24 cards | full manabase toolkit — `ManaOption` alternatives, check/pain/shock/trikelands, `pay-life-for-untapped` decision, cycling |
| P1 | 4 cards | fetch lands (OR-of-subtypes `CardFilter` + `payLife` cost) |
| P2 | 4 cards | `return-from-graveyard`, self-`mill`, `playFromGraveyard` (rule 118.9) |
| P3 | 3 cards | landfall payloads (modal token/color, `"opponent"` target) |
| P4 | 4 cards | `EffectAmount.countOf` and `.triggerValue` |
| P5 | 6 cards | `create-token` `who: target-controller`, `create-token-copy`, `conditional` effect |
| P6 | 3 cards | `AbilityCost.sacrifice: { filter }`, `on: "sacrifice"` trigger |
| P7 | 2 cards | `TriggeredAbility.condition` (intervening-if), `opponent-controls`, `sacrifice-source { then }` |
| P8 | 4 cards | `additionalCost`, `kicker`, `exile-graveyard` |
| P9 | 1 card | `flicker` effect |
| P10 | 3 cards | `{X}` in more effect positions, `selfCostReduction` |
| P11 | 2 cards | `attacks` `TriggerSpec.filter` |
| P12 | 1 card | (real text needed no new vocab) |
| P13 | 1 card | `CardFilter.notKeyword` |
| P14 | 1 card | `choose-creature-type` decision, `costModification.matchesChosenCreatureType` |
| P15 | 4 cards | `"shroud"` keyword, Exalted, `doubleEntryTriggers` (Panharmonicon effect), Saga-as-enchantment-creature |
| P16 | 9 cards | `extraLandsPerTurn`, scoped damage (`who`), `return-to-hand`/`untap` target widened to `EffectTargetRef`, live-count `costModification` |
| full-pool verify | 31 fixes | `verify-cards.mjs` — batch Scryfall diff of every pool card's stats/cost/keywords, not just new ones |
| P17 | 7 cards | untap-trigger-object, `"artifact"` target, `opponents-control-total`, `ActivatedAbility.otherOnly` |
| P18 | 3 cards + audit | re-verified every remaining `neededCards.txt` FEATURE note against real Oracle text; several were stale/wrong |
| P19 | 4 cards | live-count `selfCostReduction`, `look-and-choose` `leftover: "hand"`, `may` `then`/`else`, `ActivatedAbility.condition` |
| P20 | 1 card | `"creatures-damage-controllers"` effect, `add-mana` `{ oneOf }` form |

**Known still-blocked items from this list** (each needs a real new
primitive, not yet built, all low-count-per-item): "up to N" optional
targeting and divided damage among any number of targets (Lord Windgrace,
Dragonlord Atarka, Ureni — shared with the popularity backlog's `Tier 3`),
Rooms (a two-door card shape), Siege cycle's choose-an-ability-set-on-ETB,
Bestow (a spell that's optionally an Aura), "collect evidence" (Duskmourn's
mid-resolution cost), Station (shared with the popularity backlog above),
and a name-preserving copy exception (Sarkhan, Soul Aflame). None of these
are worth a dedicated pass alone; fold them into whichever popularity-backlog
feature above happens to unblock the same primitive.

### Controlling a player — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `mechanic:control-attack-block-choices` with
`mechanic:control-player`. Rule 723 as **who answers**, not as anything the rules
read (`player-control.ts`): the **`control-player`** effect (`target`,
`controller`, `during: "next-turn" | "next-combat"`, `extraTurnAfter`) and the
**`choose-combat`** effect (`attackers`, `blocks: "all" | "opponents"`,
`blockIfAble`). A seat answers the actions naming the player it decides for, sees
what they see (723.4), and draws the table from their seat (`view.perspective`);
bots answer through a stand-in. No new decision kind: attackers under a chooser
are the same `attackers` asked twice. `CostReductionAmount.cardTypesInGraveyard`
and `castOnlyIf: { kind: "before-attackers-declared" }` came with them. Proving
cards: Mindslaver, Worst Fears, Cruel Entertainment, Secret of Bloodbending,
Emrakul, the Promised End, Master Warcraft, Brutal Hordechief. Not covered: Word
of Command and Opposition Agent (723.2, control for a limited duration: during a
resolution, during a search, each with restrictions of its own), and a bot
playing another's turn plays nothing it needn't, one step ahead on what it's
told to decide.

### Statics on an emblem — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:emblem-triggered-abilities`, its group
`effect:emblem-static-abilities` + `effect:emblem-no-max-hand-size` +
`effect:emblem-instant-speed-loyalty`, with `effect:emblem-play-from-graveyard`
and `effect:emblem-free-cast-from-hand` riding along. An emblem with any ability
now has its object, and its statics are read with that object as their source
(`emblemStatics`, rules 113.6p, 114.4), so `create-emblem`'s `static` (and
`moreStatics`) is written as a permanent's and "you" is its owner: the layer fold
at any scope, hand size, `prohibits`, `costModification`, a `would-deal-damage`
replacement, can't-lose and can't-win, `castFromGraveyard`, `playFromGraveyard`,
`alternativeCostForSpells` (its new `fromHand`), every `activeStaticsOf` reader,
and `loyaltyAtInstantSpeed` for all its owner's planeswalkers. `emblem-statics.test.ts`
pins the fields read against the pool's emblems. Proving cards: Elspeth,
Knight-Errant, Ajani Steadfast, Teferi, Temporal Archmage, Tamiyo, the Moon Sage,
Gideon of the Trials, Jaya Ballard, Saheeli, Filigree Master. Not covered:
`effect:emblem-grants-activated` and `effect:emblem-grants-to-spells` (a grant's
activation is found by `staticIndex` on the definition, so those need a reference
to the emblem) and `effect:static-damage-life-floor-emblem`.

### Reveal until, with where the rest goes — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `effect:reveal-until-count-exile`, its group with
`effect:reveal-until-rest-bottom-any-order` and `effect:reveal-until-rest-to-hand`,
and `effect:reveal-until-count-to-hand`, `effect:reveal-until-count-tapped`,
`effect:reveal-until-rest-exile` and `effect:reveal-until-count` riding along.
`reveal-until` and `reveal-until-count` share one `rest` (`RevealedCardsGo`: bottom
random, bottom in the order chosen through the existing `choose-from-zone` order,
rule 401.4, graveyard, hand, exile, shuffle, stay). `reveal-until-count` gains
`whose`, `exile`, `put` (`"hand"`, `"with-rest"`) and `tapped`. `reveal-until`'s
`thenKeepsTargets` lets its `then` read the resolution's own targets beside the card
found. No new decision. Proving cards: Treasure Hunt, Erratic Explosion, Explosive
Revelation, Erratic Mutation, Goblin Machinist, Fathom Trawl, Auspicious Starrix,
Proteus Staff. `effect:reveal-until-count` is only partly covered: Open the Way
still needs its cap, Kindred Summons a creature type chosen on resolution, Thought
Dissector an "or X cards" stop, Indomitable Creativity one reveal per permanent
destroyed. `author-freed` narrows those.

### Skipping a turn, and what's said about an extra turn — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `condition:extra-turn`: `effect:skip-next-turn`,
`effect:extra-turn-scoped-effects` and the condition itself. `skip-turn { who?,
count? }` (rules 500.11, 614.10) leaves `PlayerState.turnsToSkip`, used up as the
turn would begin, an extra one as readily as one in the rotation; nothing that
waits for a next turn ends, since that turn never begins (614.10a). The rotation
can go all the way round when everyone owes one, and the round counts each pass.
`take-extra-turn`'s `thatTurn` holds what the effect says about that turn alone
(`GameState.extraTurnRiders`, by the queue index `extraTurnsSkippingUntap` uses):
`damageCantBePrevented` and `atEndStep`, a delayed ability set up as the turn
begins, at the new timing `"this-turn-end-step"`, so a skipped turn loses it and
one whose end step never comes lets it lapse. The `extra-turn` condition reads
`TurnState.isExtra`. No new decision; the log gets a `turn-skipped` line. Proving
cards: Meditate, Eater of Days, Wormfang Manta, Warrior's Oath, Chance for Glory,
Alchemist's Gambit, Medomai the Ageless. Not covered: Kang the Conqueror's "during
that turn, power-up abilities can't be activated", and the static "if a player
would begin an extra turn, that player skips that turn instead" (Ugin's Nexus,
Stranglehold, Trouble in Pairs, Gerrard's Hourglass Pendant).

### A number a player chooses — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 group `decision:choose-number` + `cost:choose-number-additional`:
one existing decision, `choose-number`, reached four new ways, and no new
decision kind. As an additional cost, announced like an X
(`additionalCost.chooseNumber`, the offer's `xCost.chosenNumber`). By several
players or a target opponent, open or secret and kept for later steps: a `vote`
of `for: { number }`, read by the amount `{ votedNumber }` and the `voted-number`
condition. By another player as it resolves (`choose-value`'s `who`), up to a
bound read then (`max` as an amount, with the new `{ leastOf }`). Kept on the
permanent, the last replacing the one before (`choose-value`'s `remember`, the
count `"chosen-number"` and `setBasePtFromCount.toughnessMinusCount`).
`sacrifice-all-but` now takes an amount and a player named through a target.
Proving cards: Liquid Fire, Wheel of Misfortune, Menacing Ogre, Choice of
Damnations, Diminishing Returns, Shapeshifter, Volcano Hellion. Still needing
more than this: the Clockwork creatures' "attacked or blocked this combat"
(`condition:attacked-or-blocked-this-combat`; the "up to X, total no more than
four" is `max` with `leastOf`), and Terra's token Saga copy, for `author-freed`
to read. Well of Lost Dreams and Galvanic Discharge were reachable already
(`may`'s `maxX` and `costEnergyX`). `decision:secret-choice`'s other cards are a
secret creature, player or option, not a number, so that key stays open.

### Pack tactics — built 2026-11-01

The need-keys Phase 2 family `keyword:pack-tactics`, an ability word: a
`turn-stat` condition on the new `TurnStat`
`"power-attacked-with-this-combat"`. `Game` records the total as the declaration
is made (`TurnHistory.attackedPower`, with which combat phase it was), so a pump,
a removal or a creature put onto the battlefield attacking afterwards changes
nothing, at the trigger or at its resolution (rule 603.4). A counted token stack
is every token in it. No new decision. Proving cards: Gnoll Hunter, Targ Nar,
Demon-Fang Gnoll, Battle Cry Goblin. Still needing more than this: Tiger-Tribe
Hunter's reflexive sacrifice, Minion of the Mighty's creature card from hand
tapped and attacking, Intrepid Outlander's dungeon, each for `author-freed` to
read.

**Cost reductions: new amounts, coloured, several, by target (2026-11-01,
`cost:cost-modification-extensions`).** `CostReductionAmount` gained
`excludeSelf`, `allCounters`, `colorsAmong`, `sameNameAsSpell`, `powerOf`,
`commanderManaValue`, `sum` and `scaled`, and `turnHistory` gained `who` and
`cardTypes`; the turn stat `energy-paid-or-lost` is new. `selfCostReduction` may
be a list, take coloured mana and be tied to a printed flashback (`thisWay`).
`targetCostReduction` takes coloured mana; `targetCostIncrease` is its other
half. `costModification` reads a spell's chosen targets (`targeting`,
`targetingSource`, with `perTarget`), with `Game.spellPricedByTargets` narrowing
the offers as for a spell's own reduction, and `targetedBySpellsCost` reaches a
player (`targetingYou`). `castFrom` takes a list. Proving cards: Lumbering
